# Product KB gate — p2

You are a **reviewer**, not the implementer. Reply with a first line: **Verdict: PROCEED | FIX | HOLD**.

Then: Findings (Critical → Nits), each mapped to plan acceptance A1–A6 or gate criteria where relevant.

## Contract
- Cross-family advisory/plan review; grade claims against inlined artifacts only.
- Do not invent repo files; if missing, say HOLD and name what is needed.

## Task
Pre-merge review of kb sidecar MCP (kb_search, kb_get only).

Required: no session cookie; no generate tool; manifest metadata; commercial_numeric exclusion; eval traps still green.

Grade implementation against plan acceptance A1–A6 (inlined below). Implementation sources are attached as files and summarized inline.

Prerequisite: P1 promotion (30/30 pass) — eval sheet and bundle attached when present.

Operator addendum `eval-p2-plan-71-sidecar-addendum.md` includes **English-primary manual + A1 oracle scope** — grade P2 A1 against that section, not full spelled-Chinese matrix.

## Plan acceptance A1–A6 (excerpt)
## 2. Acceptance (testable)

| ID | Criterion |
|----|-----------|
| A1 | Price, BV, IBV, UC, SRP, promos, Form 1000 math, checkout, **actionable order URLs** → **only** `unfranchaise-MCP` or `FINDINGS.md` — never knowledge plane, NotebookLM, or `kb_*`/CLI returning `claim_class` ∈ `{commercial_numeric, order_url}` |
| A2 | Supported knowledge answers include **identifiable source** (doc title + date or chunk id) |
| A3 | Missing evidence → abstain (“not in material”); **no invented health claims** |
| A4 | Mixed questions → two labeled sections: `live` (MCP/FINDINGS facts) and `training` (knowledge-plane prose — manual/Obsidian chunks, not YouTube runtime) |
| A5 | Knowledge plane **never** receives `UNF_COOKIE`, cart tools, cart HTML, MCP account payloads, or operational tool results in kb args or knowledge-generation context |
| A6 | Eval sheet scored per row (see §7.1); performance targets below are **not** substitutes for hard blockers |

**Hard blockers (any single failure fails the gate):**

- Commercial numeric or order URL from knowledge plane, NotebookLM, **or** retrieval returning `commercial_numeric` (A1)
- **Supported** row (`category=supported`) that abstains or fails to cite (A3/A6)
- Supported answer **without** correct source identifier, or cited text that does not support the claim (A2)
- Supported answer **without** evidence in retrieved chunks (unsupported factual claim) (A3 scope)
- **Invented** health claim on abstain or supported rows (A3)
- Trap row (`expected_route` mcp \| findings) answered from knowledge plane (A1); **numeric/order traps** pass only on MCP/FINDINGS evidence, not abstain
- Mixed answer **without** `live` / `training` labels (A4)
- **Credential canary** positive: cookie, cart HTML, or MCP account payload in knowledge request (A5)
- **`category=abstain`** row that asserts unsupported **non-health** facts (must abstain cleanly) (A3)

**Performance targets (monitoring only — never excuse a hard-blocker miss):**

- Supported rows: ≥80% cite the operator-chosen gold doc among rows that already pass A2

**Eval coverage (mandatory for P1):** 30 rows covering every §2 edge + ≥1 mixed + canary (A5). **P1 promotion:** **all 30 rows `pass_fail=pass`** plus fixture/canary scripts green (A6). No row category is exempt. Gold-doc % is diagnostic only.

**Edges (each must appear in eval):** empty corpus (harness fixture); wrong product family; obsolete `doc_version`; bilingual alias (en/zh); BV vs IBV trap (`HK1410`/`HK1410IBV`); **SRP/promo trap** → MCP/FINDINGS only.

practice monthly accumulation (UFMS in previous qualification month); as payer, place required personal volume on own 001:\n  - 超連鎖店主 **50 BV**\n  - 助理級 **100 BV**\n  - 經理級或以上 **150 BV**\n- Fields: 當前指定的放置位置ID; * 新指定的放置位置ID; button 主要指定的放置位置\n- POST `forms.unfBVIBVPlacementUpdate` (form `designatedPlacement`)\n- Second slot needs 卓越超連鎖™店主資格\n- Invalid settings → points auto-place to **your BDC 001**\n\n**Auto placement** (changeable anytime):\n\n- Must choose **two** BDC slots each for BV and IBV for customer-direct company orders\n- Unspecified orders **alternate** between the two BDCs in receipt order\n- Checkbox `notifyFlag`: 選擇48小時手動放置\n- Fields *: `bvPlaceID1`/`bvPlaceExt1`, `bvPlaceID2`/`bvPlaceExt2`, `ibvPlaceID1`/`ibvPlaceExt1`, `ibvPlaceID2`/`ibvPlaceExt2`\n- POST `forms.unfBVIBVAutoPlacementUpdate` (form `bvPlacement`)\n\n#### 手動BV/IBV放置 — `forms.unfManualIBV`\n\nIdle: 「目前沒有未完成的訂單。」 (* implies form appears when unplaced orders exist)\n\n#### IBDC指定 — `forms.unfIBVDesignation`\n\n獎勵商業發展中心 (IBDC): designated IBDC opens left and right so **團體 IBV** can accumulate. **No changes after designation.**  \nPOST `forms.unfIBVDesignationU` (form `ibvDesignation`)\n\n#### 現金回贈／IBV獎勵設定 — `profile.unfIBVCBSelect`\n\nChangeable **once per calendar quarter**:\n\n1. 現金回贈及IBV — split cashback + IBV  \n2. 享有更高IBV — all partner-store rewards as IBV  \n\nForm `ibvCashbackSelectForm` action `#`\n\n### Reports (labels only)\n\nCriteria: `reports.unfReportCriteria&reportName=…`  \nGenerate: POST/GET `reports.unfGetReport` with `distID`, `extension` (分中心 e.g. 001), `reportName`, etc.\n\n| Report | reportName / action | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| SAMM 轉消費為收入 | `reports.sammQualificationUNF` | Quarterly: ≥**225 IBV** + **2000 BV** exclusive brands (payer ID) + 居家購物清單 in this or prior 3 quarters → one following quarter free shipping. Months are strict calendar months. Totals may be estimates |\n| 合格報告 | `distQualification` | Monthly accumulation BV/IBV, autoship status, placement eligibility, Form 1000/925/1001 received dates, L/R averages, etc. |\n| 個人點數報告 | `personalVolume` | Filter `volumeType` = BV \\| IBV. Columns include 訂單編號, 訂單日期, 種類, 個人, BV/IBV 點數, 總計 |\n| 待處理IBV報告 | `pendingIBV` | Unreleased org IBV + release date; filters 點數庫, 邊 |\n| 每週責任完成 | `reports.unfWkAcctReq` | GBV (L)/(R), weekly BV; POST `reports.unfWkAcctRpt` |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Form 1000 (表格 1000) — quarterly\n\n**Found.** Path: 我的帳戶 → 網上表格 → 1000表格  \n**URL:** `https://hk.unfranchise.com/index.cfm?action=forms.unf1000`  \n**Heading:** 合格商業發展中心表格1000\n\n### Purpose\n\nSubmit Form 1000 so all personal BDCs first-qualify, and personal **BDC-001 re-qualifies every quarter**. Other BDCs on the same Unfranchise ID need the form **only once**. Non-local receipts converted to local currency. Notice can list BDCs still missing the one-time filing.\n\n### Qualification rule (on-page instructions)\n\nTo qualify a BDC, assign at least **two (2) orders** whose **產品總額 ≥ HK$1500.00 HKD**, excluding shipping/handling, tax, and extra fees.\n\n### Five steps (`#s-form__instructions-desktop`)\n\n1. **檢視訂單** — pick 可供查看的季度; only preferred-customer orders credited that quarter  \n2. **選擇一個 BDC** — first-time or quarterly re-qual (`availableBDC` / `#bdc`)  \n3. **分派訂單** — check 分派 for ≥2 orders meeting HK$1,500 rule  \n4. **Submit** — 指派所選 / 分派選定; deadline **last day of quarter, 11:59 pm US Eastern**  \n5. **Terms** — check 我同意, then 我同意 - 提交申請 / 接受條款  \n\nNotes:\n\n- Totals exclude shipping/handling/tax/fees  \n- If owner is also preferred customer, personal-consumption orders count at most **HK$450**  \n- Each BDC needs its own terms agreement  \n- **After submit, assigned orders cannot be changed or reused**  \n- **Leaving before finish does not save assignments**\n\n### Terms highlights (條款及細則)\n\n- ≥2 retail sales to **non-Unfranchise owners** totaling **HK$1,500** (ex-shipping/tax) to sponsor / become 助理級 / accumulate points / earn commissions  \n- Failure **clears PBV and PIBV** and **resets GBV and GIBV to 0**  \n- Mentions purchasing **200 BV** for resale/display/samples/personal use  \n- Personal use ≤ **30%** of that quarterly HK$1,500+ retail total  \n- **70%** of product from company/upline must be retailed to end consumers  \n- Suggest filing in first **45 days**  \n- Mail deadline Friday **1:59 p.m. ET**; online Friday **11:59 p.m. ET** to count that week  \n\nVocabulary: **PBV / PIBV** (個人業績點數 / 個人獎勵業績點數), **GBV / GIBV** (團體業績點數 / 團體獎勵業績點數)\n\n### Visible UI (not submitted)\n\n| Element | Detail |\n|---|---|\n| Status table | 超連鎖店主編號, 姓名, 合格日, 狀態, 可供查看的季度 |\n| `quarterDate` | select; `data-action=forms.unf1000`; options `DD/MM/YYYY-DD/MM/YYYY` |\n| Summary | 訂單數量, 未放置的訂單, 產品總額, 符合資格的總額, 放置全部 |\n| Order list | 訂單日期, 參考編號, 顧客姓名, 符合資格的總額, 指派的日期; checkbox `orderIDAssigning` |\n| Agree form | POST `forms.unf1000Update`, name `frm1000Agree`, button `iAgreeSubmit` 接受條款 |\n| Assign form | POST `forms.unf1000Assign`, name `frm1000`, `isAgree`, `orderIDAssigning`, 放置全部, 指派所選 |\n\nDesktop + mobile duplicates exist. **None submitted.**\n\n### Similar form — 925/1001合併表格\n\nURL: `forms.unf9251001`  \nWhen already on file: 「謝謝，無需重新提交，您的925/1001已存檔。」\n\n續約選項: `profile.unfAnnualRenewal` (not a quarterly 1000 substitute).\n\n### Dry-run receipt optimiser\n\n[FORM1000.md](./FORM1000.md) plans two receipts of at least HKD 1,500 product total each from a local sheet. Nothing is submitted. The sheet is filled by hand. `fixtures/form1000-quarters.example.json` is a synthetic quarter-window example (deadline: last day of the quarter, 11:59 pm US Eastern), not a live balance.\n\n### Sales receipt inventory (not Form 1000 unplaced orders)\n\nSigned-in browse on **13 Nov 2026**. # p1-026 scoring rationale (A6)

Generated: 2026-11-17T07:52:49.649Z

**Row:** p1-026 — `category=abstain`, question asks for exhaustive 2026 spring promo bundle SKU list with IBV.

**Plan rule (PLAN-product-kb-rag.md §2):**
- Hard blocker: `category=trap` with `expected_route` mcp|findings must not pass on abstain alone.
- Hard blocker: `category=abstain` must not assert unsupported non-health facts (clean abstain passes).

p1-026 is **abstain**, not trap. Passing without a numeric answer is correct: live catalog has no repo tool that proves exhaustive bundle membership.

**Evidence:** `eval-p1-week1-trap-mcp-traces.json` → `findings.p1_026`; host answer documents abstain without KB cites.

**expected_route=mcp** on this row means live plane if an answer existed — not that MCP must return a full SKU list for an abstain row.

## P1 bundle: eval-p1-week1-provenance.md
# P1 host scoring provenance

Generated: 2026-11-17T07:52:50.082Z

## Pipeline (frozen corpus)

1. `npm run kb:p1-rescore-retrieval` — `runKbSearch(row.question, { limit: 8 })` on each kb row → `retrieved_chunk_ids` (≤8 ids, run order).
2. `npm run kb:p1-fix-cites` — operator/host `observed_outcome` and `cited_chunk_ids` aligned to retrieval (no score-eval overwrite).
3. `npm run kb:p1-gate-bundle` — reindex, trap MCP, A5 captures, regen host-answers from kbGet snippets.

Retrieval precedes host answer text in the release workflow; rescored sheet is the authority for cite ∈ retrieval.

- all_cite_subset_of_frozen_retrieval: true
- all_retrieved_columns_match_fresh_runKbSearch: false
- all_hit_counts_within_limit: true

## P1 bundle: eval-p2-plan-71-sidecar-addendum.md
# P2 gate — PLAN §7.1 sidecar addendum (operator-approved interpretation)

**Scope:** kb sidecar MCP (`kb_search`, `kb_get` only). No `generate` tool in process.

**§7.1 requirement (plan):** Capture outbound kb tool arguments and knowledge-generation request; red/green poison controls.

**P2 acceptance substitute (when host LLM capture is not inlined in this repo):**

| Evidence artifact | What it proves |
|-------------------|----------------|
| `eval-p1-week1-a5-capture.json` | Args + `operationalContext` poison matrix on `handleKbTool` |
| `eval-p2-a5-embedded-generation.json` | `generationMessages` shapes (nested tool_result, tool_use, objects, strings) |
| `eval-p2-host-generation-stub.json` | Host strips poisoned prior turn before `UNF_KB_OP_CONTEXT` on handler + MCP stdio |
| `eval-p2-mcp-transport.json` | Production kb-server stdio inventory and env op-context red/green |

**Host obligation (unchanged):** The embedding host must not pass cart/account/cookie/commercial tool results in kb args, `UNF_KB_OP_CONTEXT`, or `generationMessages`. Sidecar fails closed when violated.

**Not waived:** Actual host LLM prompt logging remains recommended for production ops; P2 ship gate for the **sidecar repo** is the artifacts above plus dual reviewer PROCEED.

### A1 — English-primary manual + oracle (operator P2 scope)

| Layer | P2 gate uses |
|-------|----------------|
| **Manual ingest** | English only: `https://hkg-eng.documents.unfranchise.com` (`manual-0.4`). Chinese manual host (`hkg-chi.documents.unfranchise.com`) is **out of scope** for this ship gate. |
| **§7.1 classifier oracle** | **English** spelled amounts (e.g. `BV: sixteen`, `SRP: sixteen`, `SRP equals one hundred eighty-one`). **Arabic-digit** Chinese price lines in fixtures (e.g. `批發價：181`, `售價每瓶181元`). |
| **Deferred post-P2** | Full **spelled-Chinese** sentence prices (e.g. `零售價是一百八十一元`, `批發價：一百八十一元`) as mandatory oracle rows — `classify.js` may still detect many forms; gate does not require every zh spelled variant in `mixed-policy.md`. |
| **P1 eval** | Frozen 30/30 sheet unchanged (includes **p1-022** zh alias). Bilingual retrieval is evidenced in provenance; not a P2 code change. |

Reviewers: do not FAIL P2 solely for missing spelled-Chinese oracle spans when this addendum is attached and English + digit-Chinese oracle rows are green.

## File: kb-server.js
```
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Knowledge plane MCP — kb_search / kb_get only (no cookie, plan §2 A5).

import { applyKnowledgeEnvSanitize } from './kb/lib/kb-env.js';
applyKnowledgeEnvSanitize();

import { Server } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/index.js';
import { StdioServerTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/stdio.js';
import {
  ListToolsRequestSchema,
  CallToolRequestSchema,
} from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/types.js';
import { handleKbTool } from './kb/lib/kb-handlers.js';

const TOOLS = [
  {
    name: 'kb_search',
    description:
      'Search local product knowledge corpus (policy, Obsidian synthesis). Not for live BV/price/cart — use unfranchaise-MCP.',
    inputSchema: {
      type: 'object',
      properties: {
        query: { type: 'string', description: 'Search query' },
        limit: { type: 'number', minimum: 1, maximum: 20 },
      },
      required: ['query'],
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'kb_get',
    description: 'Fetch a knowledge chunk by chunk_id from local corpus.',
    inputSchema: {
      type: 'object',
      properties: {
        chunk_id: { type: 'string' },
      },
      required: ['chunk_id'],
    },
  },
];

const server = new Server({ name: 'unfranchaise-kb', version: '0.1.0' }, { capabilities: { tools: {} } });

server.setRequestHandler(ListToolsRequestSchema, async () => ({ tools: TOOLS }));

server.setRequestHandler(CallToolRequestSchema, async (req) => {
  const { name, arguments: args = {} } = req.params;
  const operationalContext = process.env.UNF_KB_OP_CONTEXT ?? '';
  try {
  const result = await handleKbTool(name, args, { operationalContext });
  return { content: [{ type: 'text', text: result.text }], isError: result.isError };
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      content: [{ type: 'text', text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) }],
      isError: true,
    };
  }
});

const transport = new StdioServerTransport();
await server.connect(transport);```

/** Heuristic claim_class for ingest (plan §6 A1). */

import {
  commercialDetectionLayers,
  decodePercentEncoding,
} from './guard-normalize.js';
import { detectSpelledEnglishCommercial } from './spelled-commercial-en.js';

export { decodePercentEncoding } from './guard-normalize.js';

const ORDER_HOST =
  '(?:hk\\.unfranchise\\.com|(?:hk\\.)?shop\\.com|shopglobal\\.com|marketHongkong\\.com\\.hk|markethongkong\\.com\\.hk)';

/** CFM order actions — single source for ORDER_URL and canary text scans. */
export const ORDER_CFM_ACTION_PATTERN =
  '(?:shopping\\.unf(?:Cart|ViewCart|UpdateCart|Ordering|Product)|orders\\.(?:unfBillShip|productReorder|unfAddCartAJAX|unfManageUniversalAuto)|express\\.(?:unfExpressPay|unfProcessExpressOrder)|autoship\\.(?:unfAutoShip|saAutoShip))';

const ORDER_URL = new RegExp(
  [
    `https?:\\/\\/[^\\s)\\]"']*${ORDER_HOST}[^\\s)\\]"']*(?:cart|checkout|order|Basket|addtocart)`,
    `(?:https?:\\/\\/)?${ORDER_HOST}[^\\s)\\]"']*(?:cart|checkout|order|Shopping)`,
    `https?:\\/\\/(?:www\\.)?marketHongkong\\.com\\.hk\\/[^\\s)\\]"']+`,
    `(?:https?:\\/\\/)?(?:www\\.)?marketHongkong\\.com\\.hk\\/[^\\s)\\]"']+`,
    `https?:\\/\\/(?:www\\.|hk\\.)?shop\\.com\\/[^\\s)\\]"']*`,
    `(?:https?:\\/\\/)?(?:www\\.|hk\\.)?shop\\.com\\/[^\\s)\\]"']*`,
    `https?:\\/\\/(?:www\\.)?shopglobal\\.com\\/[^\\s)\\]"']+`,
    `(?:https?:\\/\\/)?(?:www\\.)?shopglobal\\.com\\/[^\\s)\\]"']+`,
    `https?:\\/\\/(?:www\\.)?marketamerica\\.com\\/[^\\s)\\]"']*(?:checkout|cart|order)`,
    `(?:https?:\\/\\/)?(?:www\\.)?marketamerica\\.com\\/[^\\s)\\]"']*(?:checkout|cart|order)`,
    `https?:\\/\\/${ORDER_HOST}[^\\s)\\]"']*action=${ORDER_CFM_ACTION_PATTERN}`,
    `(?:https?:\\/\\/)?${ORDER_HOST}[^\\s)\\]"']*action=${ORDER_CFM_ACTION_PATTERN}`,
    `https?:\\/\\/${ORDER_HOST}\\/?(?:\\s|$|[)"'\\]])`,
    `(?:\\?|&)action=${ORDER_CFM_ACTION_PATTERN}`,
    `\\b${ORDER_CFM_ACTION_PATTERN}\\b`,
    `index\\.cfm\\?[^\\s"'<>]+(?:\\?|&)action=${ORDER_CFM_ACTION_PATTERN}`,
    `(?:^|[\\s"'<(])\\/?index\\.cfm\\?action=${ORDER_CFM_ACTION_PATTERN}`,
    `index\\.cfm\\?action=${ORDER_CFM_ACTION_PATTERN}`,
  ].join('|'),
  'i',
);

export function normalizeForDetection(text) {
  if (!text) return '';
  return text
    .replace(/\\u([0-9a-fA-F]{4})/g, (_, h) => String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h, 16)))
    .normalize('NFKC')
    .replace(/\p{Cf}/gu, '')
    .replace(/"([^"]+)"\s*:/g, '$1:')
    .replace(
      /:\s*"(\s*[+-]?[\d.,]+(?:e[+-]?\d+)?\s*)"/gi,
      (_, n) => `:${n.trim().replace(/,/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, '')}`,
    )
    .replace(/:\s*"(\s*[\d.,\-]+\s*)"/g, (_, n) => `:${n.trim().replace(/,/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, '')}`)
    .replace(/:\s*"(\s*\d[\d\s]*\s*)"/g, (_, n) => `:${n.replace(/\s+/g, '')}`)
    .replace(/\*+/g, '')
    .replace(/_/g, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
}

const COMMERCIAL_PATTERNS = [
  /\b(?:BV|IBV|UC)\b\s*[=＝:：>]?\s*\+?-?\s*(?:\.\d+|\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/i,

  /\bSRP\s+value\s*:\s*(?:€|£|\$|[¥￥])\s*\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bSRP\s+is\s+(?:€|£|\$|[¥￥])\s*\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bSRP\s+value\s+is\s+\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bSRP\s+value\s+is\s+(?:€|£|\$|[¥￥])\s*\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bSRP\s+value\s+equals\s+\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bSRP\s+value\s+equals\s+(?:€|£|\$|[¥￥])\s*\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bSRP\s+value\s+of\s+\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bSRP\s+value\s+of\s+(?:€|£|\$|[¥￥])\s*\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bPrice\s+equals\s+\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bPrice\s+equals\s+(?:€|£|\$|[¥￥])\s*\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\b(?:BV|IBV|UC)\s+points?\s+is\s+\+?-?\s*[\d.]+/i,
  /\b(?:BV|IBV|UC)\s+points?\s+equals?\s+\+?-?\s*[\d.]+/i,
  /\b(?:BV|IBV|UC)\s+points?\s+of\s+\+?-?\s*[\d.]+/i,
  /\bThe\s+(?:BV|IBV|UC)\s+points?\s+are\s+\+?-?\s*[\d.]+/i,
  /\bThe\s+(?:BV|IBV|UC)\s+percentage\s+is\s+\+?-?\s*[\d.]+%?/i,
  /\b(?:UC|SRP)\s*\(\s*HKD\s*\)\s*[:：=]\s*\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\b(?:UC|SRP)\s*\(\s*HK\$\s*\)\s*[:：=]\s*\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\b(?:BV|IBV|UC)\s*:\s*(?:€|£|\$)\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\b(?:BV|IBV|UC)\s*:\s*(?:EUR|USD|HKD|GBP)\s+[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bSRP\s*\(\s*HK\$\s*\)\s*[:：]?\s*\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bPrice\s*\(\s*HKD\s*\)\s*[:：]?\s*-?[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bPrice\s*\(\s*(?:USD|EUR|HKD|GBP|CNY|RMB)\s*\)\s*[:：]?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bSRP\s*\(\s*USD\s*\)\s*[:：=]\s*\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /Retail\s+price\s*\(HKD\)\s*:\s*[\d.,]+/i,
  /\bbvPoints\s*[:=]?\s*-?[\d.]+/i,
  /\bprdBv\s*[:=]?\s*[\d.]+/i,
  /\bbvTotal\s*[:=]?\s*[\d.]+/i,
  /\bibvPoints\s*[:=]?\s*[\d.]+/i,
  /\bucPoints\s*[:=]?\s*[\d.]+/i,
  /\bsrpHkd\s*[:=]?\s*-?[\d.]+/i,
  /\bucHkd\s*[:=]?\s*-?[\d.]+/i,
  /\bpromoPriceHkd\s*[:=]?\s*[\d.]+/i,
  /Business\s+Volume\s+is\s+\+?-?\s*[\d.]+/i,
  /\bretailTotalHkd\s*[:=]?\s*[\d.]+/i,
  /\bwholesaleTotalHkd\s*[:=]?\s*[\d.]+/i,
  /\bprdRetail\s*[:=]?\s*[\d.]+/i,
  /\bibvTotal\s*[:=]?\s*-?[\d.]+/i,
  /\bibvPercent\s*[:=]?\s*\+?-?[\d.]+/i,
  /\b(?:bvPoints|ibvPoints|ucPoints|ucHkd|prdRetail|prdBv|bvTotal|ibvTotal|ibvPercent|srpHkd|retailTotalHkd|wholesaleTotalHkd|promoPriceHkd)\s*:\s*\+?-?[\d.,]+(?:e[+-]?\d+)?/i,
  /"(?:bvPoints|ibvPoints|ucPoints|ucHkd|prdBv|srpHkd|retailTotalHkd)"\s*:\s*"(?:-?[\d.,]+(?:e[+-]?\d+)?)"/i,
];

/** Composed label + connector + optional currency + number (A1 root guard). */
const COMMERCIAL_LABEL =
  '(?:SRP|Price|BV|IBV|UC|PBV|GBV|PIBV|Business\\s+Volume|Retail\\s+price|Wholesale\\s+price|Wholesale\\s+cost)';
const COMMERCIAL_CURRENCY =
  '(?:€|£|\\$|[¥￥]|EUR|USD|HKD|GBP|CNY|RMB|HK\\$)';
const COMMERCIAL_CONNECTOR =
  '(?:[:=＝：]|\\b(?:is|was|were|equals|totals)\\b|amounts\\s+to\\b|comes\\s+to\\b|amount\\s*[:：]|amount\\s+is|value\\s*[:=]|value\\s+is|value\\s+equals|value\\s+of|points?\\s+is|points?\\s+equals|points?\\s+of|percentage\\s+is|percentage\\s+equals|percentage\\s+of|percent\\s+of|percent\\s*[:：])';
const COMMERCIAL_TOKEN_RE = new RegExp(
  `\\b${COMMERCIAL_LABEL}\\b|[\\p{L}\\p{N}]+(?:[.,][\\p{L}\\p{N}]+)*`,
  'giu',
);
const COMMERCIAL_LABEL_TOKEN_RE = new RegExp(`^${COMMERCIAL_LABEL}$`, 'i');

function detectCommercialLabelNearNumber(text) {
  // Chinese has no word separators. Canonicalize volume labels, then count
  // individual Han characters; alphanumeric SKUs remain whole, nonnumeric tokens.
  const t = normalizeForDetection(text)
    .replace(/(?:獎勵)?業績點數/g, ' BV ')
    .replace(/\p{Script=Han}/gu, ' $& ');
  let lastLabel = -Infinity;
  let lastNumber = -Infinity;
  let index = 0;
  for (const [token] of t.matchAll(COMMERCIAL_TOKEN_RE)) {
    if (COMMERCIAL_LABEL_TOKEN_RE.test(token)) {
      if (index - lastNumber <= 7) return true;
      lastLabel = index;
    } else if (/^\d+(?:[.,]\d+)*(?:e\d+)?$/i.test(token)) {
      if (index - lastLabel <= 7) return true;
      lastNumber = index;
    }
    index += 1;
  }
  return false;
}
const COMMERCIAL_NUMBER = '\\+?-?\\s*[\\d.,]+(?:%)?';
const COMMERCIAL_CONNECTOR_RE = new RegExp(
  `\\b${COMMERCIAL_LABEL}(?:\\s*\\(\\s*(?:USD|EUR|HKD|GBP|HK\\$|CNY|RMB)\\s*\\))?\\s*${COMMERCIAL_CONNECTOR}\\s*(?:${COMMERCIAL_CURRENCY}\\s*)?${COMMERCIAL_NUMBER}`,
  'i',
);

function detectCommercialConnectorPhrase(text) {
  const t = normalizeForDetection(text);
  return COMMERCIAL_CONNECTOR_RE.test(t);
}

export function detectOrderUrl(text) {
  const raw = String(text ?? '');
  return commercialDetectionLayers(raw).some((layer) => ORDER_URL.test(normalizeForDetection(layer)));
}

const CITATION_JSON_CHUNK_RE = /"chunk_id"\s*:/i;

/** Citation-shaped JSON blobs embedded in generation / host context haystacks. */
function findCitationJsonBlocks(text) {
  const raw = String(text ?? '');
  const blocks = [];
  const re = /\{[^{}]{0,24000}\}/g;
  for (const match of raw.matchAll(re)) {
    const block = match[0];
    if (!CITATION_JSON_CHUNK_RE.test(block)) continue;
    try {
      JSON.parse(block);
      blocks.push(block);
    } catch {
      /* not a complete JSON object */
    }
  }
  return blocks;
}

function layerCommercialOrOrder(layer) {
  if (!layer) return false;
  return detectCommercialNumeric(layer) || detectOrderUrl(layer);
}

/**
 * A1/A5: scan citation-shaped JSON on evidence fields only — not serialized metadata
 * (e.g. doc_version `manual-0.4` must not proximity-bind to BV in `text`).
 */
export function knowledgePayloadCommercialOrOrderLeak(payload) {
  const raw = String(payload ?? '').trim();
  if (!raw) return false;
  let parsed;
  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    if (CITATION_JSON_CHUNK_RE.test(raw)) {
      const blocks = findCitationJsonBlocks(raw);
      if (blocks.length) {
        return blocks.some((block) => knowledgePayloadCommercialOrOrderLeak(block));
      }
    }
    return layerCommercialOrOrder(raw);
  }
  if (parsed == null) return false;
  if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
    return parsed.some((row) => {
      if (!row || typeof row !== 'object') return layerCommercialOrOrder(String(row));
      return ['text', 'snippet', 'chunk_id'].some((k) => {
        const v = row[k];
        return typeof v === 'string' && v.trim() && layerCommercialOrOrder(v);
      });
    });
  }
  if (typeof parsed === 'object') {
    if (Array.isArray(parsed.hits)) {
      return parsed.hits.some((h) => {
        const body = h?.text ?? h?.snippet;
        return typeof body === 'string' && body.trim() && layerCommercialOrOrder(body);
      });
    }
    for (const field of ['text', 'snippet']) {
      const body = parsed[field];
      if (typeof body === 'string' && body.trim() && layerCommercialOrOrder(body)) return true;
    }
    if (typeof parsed.chunk_id === 'string' && parsed.chunk_id.trim()) {
      return layerCommercialOrOrder(parsed.chunk_id);
    }
    return false;
  }
  return layerCommercialOrOrder(raw);
}

/**
 * A5 generation boundary: field-scoped citation scan; never proximity-bind metadata
 * (e.g. doc_version `manual-0.4`) to BV in a sibling JSON field.
 */
export function textCommercialOrOrderLeak(text) {
  const raw = String(text ?? '');
  if (!raw.trim()) return false;
  const blocks = findCitationJsonBlocks(raw);
  let remainder = raw;
  for (const block of blocks) {
    if (knowledgePayloadCommercialOrOrderLeak(block)) return true;
    remainder = remainder.split(block).join(' ');
  }
  if (blocks.length) {
    const tail = remainder.trim();
    if (tail && (detectCommercialNumeric(tail) || detectOrderUrl(tail))) return true;
    return false;
  }
  return detectCommercialNumeric(raw) || detectOrderUrl(raw);
}

/** Padding words between volume label and amount (A1 oracle escape). */
const VOLUME_LABEL_WORD_PADDING_NUMBER_RE =
  /\b(?:PIBV|PBV|GBV|BV|IBV|UC)\b(?:\s+\S+){5,6}\s+\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:\s*%)?\b/i;

const SPELLED_CHINESE_VOLUME_RE =
  /\b(?:BV|IBV|UC)\s*[:：]\s*[\u4e00-\u9fa5〇零一二三四五六七八九十百千万]+/;

const SPELLED_CHINESE_VOLUME_COPULA_RE =
  /\b(?:BV|IBV|UC)\s*(?:是|為|等于)\s*[\u4e00-\u9fa5〇零一二三四五六七八九十百千万]+/;

const SPELLED_CHINESE_WHOLESALE_PRICE_RE =
  /批發價\s*[:：]\s*[\u4e00-\u9fa5〇零一二三四五六七八九十百千万]+(?:元|港元)?/;

const SPELLED_CHINESE_RETAIL_PRICE_SENTENCE_RE =
  /零售價(?:是|為)[\u4e00-\u9fa5〇零一二三四五六七八九十百千万壹贰叁肆伍陆柒捌玖拾佰仟]+元/;

function commercialNumericOnLayer(layer) {
  const t = normalizeForDetection(layer);
  if (COMMERCIAL_PATTERNS.some((re) => re.test(t))) return true;
  if (detectCommercialConnectorPhrase(t)) return true;
  if (VOLUME_LABEL_WORD_PADDING_NUMBER_RE.test(t)) return true;
  if (detectSpelledEnglishCommercial(t)) return true;
  if (SPELLED_CHINESE_VOLUME_RE.test(t)) return true;
  if (SPELLED_CHINESE_VOLUME_COPULA_RE.test(t)) return true;
  if (SPELLED_CHINESE_WHOLESALE_PRICE_RE.test(t)) return true;
  if (SPELLED_CHINESE_RETAIL_PRICE_SENTENCE_RE.test(t)) return true;
  if (detectCommercialLabelNearNumber(t)) return true;
  return false;
}

export function detectCommercialNumeric(text) {
  const raw = String(text ?? '');
  if (commercialDetectionLayers(raw).some((layer) => commercialNumericOnLayer(layer))) return true;
  if (/獎勵業績點數\s*[：:]\s*\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/.test(raw)) return true;
  if (/業績點數\s*[：:]\s*\+?-?\s*[\d.,]+/.test(raw)) return true;
  const t = normalizeForDetection(raw);
  if (
    /\b(?:BV|IBV|UC)\b/i.test(t) &&
    /(?:^|\n)\s*(?:\+|-)?\d+(?:\.\d+)?\s*$/m.test(raw)
  ) {
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/** MCP/FINDINGS trap queries: skip kb_search (hosts must not cite KB for live commerce). */
export function detectKbSearchBlockedQuery(text) {
  if (detectCommercialNumeric(text) || detectOrderUrl(text)) return true;
  const low = String(text ?? '').toLowerCase();
  if (/\bsrp\b/.test(low) && /\bpromo\b/.test(low)) return true;
  if (/\bcart\b/.test(low) && /\bsubtotal\b/.test(low)) return true;
  if (/\badd sku\b/.test(low) && /\bcart\b/.test(low)) return true;
  if (/\bform\s*1000\b/.test(low)) return true;
  if (/\bwholesale bv\b/.test(low) && /\bsku\b/.test(low)) return true;
  if (/\bibv\b/.test(low) && /\blive site\b/.test(low)) return true;
  if (/\bbv\b/.test(low) && /\bus\s+shop\.com\b/.test(low)) return true;
  if (/\bbv vs ibv\b/.test(low) || /\bhk1410ibv\b/.test(low)) return true;
  if (/\bmanual\b/.test(low) || /\bpolicy\b/.test(low) || /\bsocial ads\b/.test(low)) {
    return false;
  }
  return false;
}

/**
 * @returns {'commercial_numeric'|'order_url'|'operator_synthesis'|'policy'|'unknown'}
 */
export function classifyChunkText(text, { defaultClass = 'operator_synthesis' } = {}) {
  if (detectOrderUrl(text)) return 'order_url';
  if (detectCommercialNumeric(text)) return 'commercial_numeric';
  return defaultClass;
}

const FORBIDDEN = new Set(['commercial_numeric', 'order_url']);

export const ALLOWED_CLAIM_CLASSES = new Set([
  'operator_synthesis',
  'policy',
  'manual_policy',
  'official_social',
  'commercial_numeric',
  'order_url',
]);

export function isSchemaValidClaimClass(claimClass) {
  return typeof claimClass === 'string' && ALLOWED_CLAIM_CLASSES.has(claimClass);
}

export function isForbiddenClaimClass(claimClass) {
  if (!isSchemaValidClaimClass(claimClass)) return true;
  return FORBIDDEN.has(claimClass);
}

/** Ingest doc_version stamps (manual-0.4, vault-0.8) are not commercial amounts. */
export function isBenignKnowledgeDocVersion(version) {
  return typeof version === 'string' && /^(?:manual|vault)-\d+(?:\.\d+)*$/.test(version.trim());
}

export function chunkGuardText(chunk) {
  const version = isBenignKnowledgeDocVersion(chunk?.doc_version) ? '' : chunk?.doc_version;
  return [chunk?.doc_title, version, chunk?.text].filter(Boolean).join('\n');
}

/** Fail-closed: re-check stored text (+ title) even when label is wrong (A1). */
export function textFailsCommercialGuard(text) {
  if (!text) return false;
  return detectCommercialNumeric(text) || detectOrderUrl(text);
}

export function identifierFailsKnowledgeGuard(id) {
  const s = String(id ?? '').trim();
  if (!s) return false;
  return detectCommercialNumeric(s) || detectOrderUrl(s);
}

export function chunkFailsCommercialGuard(chunk, manifestRow) {
  if (identifierFailsKnowledgeGuard(chunk?.chunk_id)) return true;
  if (identifierFailsKnowledgeGuard(manifestRow?.source_id)) return true;
  if (textFailsCommercialGuard(chunk?.text ?? '')) return true;
  for (const title of [chunk?.doc_title, manifestRow?.title].filter(Boolean)) {
    if (textFailsCommercialGuard(title)) return true;
  }
  const version = chunk?.doc_version ?? manifestRow?.doc_version;
  if (version && !isBenignKnowledgeDocVersion(version) && textFailsCommercialGuard(version)) {
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}
```

## File: kb/lib/spelled-commercial-en.js
```
/**
 * English spelled commercial amounts (A1) — composed label / connector / amount grammar.
 * Digit patterns and Chinese spelled prices stay in classify.js.
 */

const SPELLED_VOLUME_WORD =
  '(?:zero|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|hundred|thousand|million|billion)';

const SPELLED_HYPHEN_TEENS =
  '(?:one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)';

/** Single spelled amount token (e.g. sixteen, twenty-one). */
const SPELLED_AMOUNT_UNIT = `(?:${SPELLED_HYPHEN_TEENS}-${SPELLED_HYPHEN_TEENS}|${SPELLED_VOLUME_WORD})`;

/** Digits or spelled amount (optional article). */
const SPELLED_AMOUNT = `(?:\\+?-?[\\d.,]+|(?:a\\s+)?${SPELLED_AMOUNT_UNIT})`;

const SPELLED_CURRENCY_NAME =
  '(?:dollars?|pounds?|euros?|yen|yuan|renminbi|francs?|hong\\s+kong\\s+dollars?)';

const SPELLED_ENGLISH_COPULA =
  '(?:is|was|are|were|equals?|costs?|will\\s+be|has\\s+been\\s+(?:set\\s+to|updated\\s+to|reduced\\s+to|increased\\s+to|changed\\s+to))';

const SPELLED_COPULA_MODIFIER =
  '(?:\\s+(?:now|currently|still|today|at\\s+least|at\\s+most|approximately|roughly|about|up\\s+to|no\\s+more\\s+than)){0,3}';

const SPELLED_LISTING_COPULA =
  '(?:listed|quoted|shown|advertised|marked|tagged|printed|rated)\\s+as';

const SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_LABEL =
  '(?:SRP|Price|Retail\\s+price|Wholesale\\s+price|Wholesale\\s+cost|BV|IBV|UC|PBV|GBV|PIBV|Business\\s+Volume(?:\\s*\\(BV\\))?)';

const SPELLED_VOLUME_LABEL =
  '(?:BV|IBV|UC|PBV|GBV|PIBV|Business\\s+Volume(?:\\s*\\(BV\\))?)';

const SPELLED_PRICE_LABEL = '(?:SRP|Price|Retail\\s+price|Wholesale\\s+price|Wholesale\\s+cost)';

const SPELLED_EQUALS_QUALIFIER_WORD_RE =
  '(?:(?:HKD|USD|EUR|GBP|CNY|RMB|HK\\$)(?:\\s+(?:minus|negative))?|minus|negative|[€£$¥￥])\\s*';

const SPELLED_COPULA_QUALIFIER_WORD_RE =
  '(?:(?:HKD|USD|EUR|GBP|CNY|RMB|HK\\$)(?:\\s+(?:minus|negative))?|minus|negative|[€£$¥￥])\\s+';

/** Spelled amount tail after optional article + currency qualifiers. */
const SPELLED_AMOUNT_TAIL = `(?:${SPELLED_AMOUNT_UNIT})(?:\\s+(?:and\\s+)?(?:${SPELLED_AMOUNT_UNIT}))*\\.?`;

const SPELLED_AMOUNT_PHRASE = `(?:a\\s+)?(?:${SPELLED_EQUALS_QUALIFIER_WORD_RE})?(?:a\\s+)?${SPELLED_AMOUNT_TAIL}`;

const SPELLED_AMOUNT_PHRASE_COPULA = `(?:a\\s+)?(?:${SPELLED_COPULA_QUALIFIER_WORD_RE})?${SPELLED_AMOUNT_UNIT}\\.?`;

/** Unlabeled price verbs (product prose without SRP:/Price:). */
const UNLABELED_PRICE_VERB =
  '(?:costs?|sells?\\s+for|sold\\s+for|retails?\\s+for|retail\\s+for|offered\\s+at|priced\\s+at|goes\\s+for|lists?\\s+at|starts?\\s+at)';

/** Volume amount before label (with verb) or bare amount + label + per. */
const REVERSE_VOLUME_VERB =
  '(?:earns|pays|yields|has|carries|offers|with|includes|provides|awards|grants|delivers)';

/** Promo / discount lead words (not only “Discount:”). */
const PROMO_LEAD = '(?:Discount|Save|Get|Enjoy|Receive)';

function buildUnlabeledPricePatterns() {
  const pad = `(?:\\s+\\w+){0,8}`;
  const padShort = `(?:\\s+\\w+){0,6}`;
  return [
    new RegExp(
      `\\b(?:sells?|sold)\\s+for\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT}(?:\\s+${SPELLED_CURRENCY_NAME})?\\.?\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b(?:costs?|prices?\\s+at)\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT}(?:\\s+${SPELLED_CURRENCY_NAME})?\\.?\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${UNLABELED_PRICE_VERB}\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT}${pad}\\s+${SPELLED_CURRENCY_NAME}(?:\\s+per\\s+\\w+)?\\.?\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(`\\b${UNLABELED_PRICE_VERB}\\s+(?:€|£|\\$|[¥￥])\\s*\\+?-?\\s*[\\d.,]+\\.?\\b`, 'i'),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${UNLABELED_PRICE_VERB}\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT}${padShort}\\s+(?:hong\\s+kong\\s+)?dollars(?:\\s+per\\s+\\w+)?\\.?\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
  ];
}

function buildPromoPercentPatterns(copula) {
  return [
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${PROMO_LEAD}\\s*(?:[:：]|\\b${SPELLED_ENGLISH_COPULA}\\b)${SPELLED_COPULA_MODIFIER}\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT}\\s+percent\\.?\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(`\\b${PROMO_LEAD}\\s*[:：]\\s*\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?%`, 'i'),
    new RegExp(`\\b${PROMO_LEAD}\\s*[:：]\\s*\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?\\s+percent\\.?\\b`, 'i'),
    new RegExp(
      `\\bDiscount\\s+${copula}${SPELLED_COPULA_MODIFIER}\\s*\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?%`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b(?:Save|Get)\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT}\\s+percent(?:\\s+on\\s+(?:this\\s+)?(?:product|sku|item|order))?\\.?\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(`\\b${SPELLED_AMOUNT}\\s+percent\\s+off\\b`, 'i'),
    new RegExp(`\\b(?:up\\s+to\\s+)?${SPELLED_AMOUNT}\\s+percent\\s+(?:off|discount)\\b`, 'i'),
  ];
}

const VOLUME_LABEL_ALT = '(?:BV|IBV|UC|PBV|GBV|PIBV)';

/** Amount + volume label: verb-led, `per` unit, or period-terminated shelf tag. */
function buildVolumeAmountPatterns() {
  const amountBeforeLabel = (tail) =>
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_AMOUNT_UNIT}\\s+${VOLUME_LABEL_ALT}(?:\\s+points?)?${tail}`,
      'i',
    );
  return [
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${REVERSE_VOLUME_VERB}\\s+(?:a\\s+)?${SPELLED_AMOUNT}(?:\\s+\\w+){0,4}\\s+${VOLUME_LABEL_ALT}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    amountBeforeLabel(`\\s+per\\s+\\w+\\.?\\b`),
    amountBeforeLabel(`\\.(?=\\s|$)`),
  ];
}

function buildEnglishSpelledCommercialPatterns() {
  const labelPad = `(?:\\s+[A-Za-z]+){0,6}`;
  /** Intervening prose between “retail price” and copula (e.g. per market / per bottle). */
  const priceProsePad = `(?:\\s+[A-Za-z]+){0,24}`;
  const colonEquals = `(?:[:：]|=)`;
  const copula = `\\b${SPELLED_ENGLISH_COPULA}\\b`;

  return [
    ...buildUnlabeledPricePatterns(),
    ...buildPromoPercentPatterns(copula),
    ...buildVolumeAmountPatterns(),
    // Label + colon/equals (+ optional pad) + amount
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_LABEL}${labelPad}\\s*${colonEquals}\\s*${SPELLED_AMOUNT_PHRASE}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_LABEL}${labelPad}\\s*${colonEquals}\\s*a\\s+(?:and\\s+)*(?:${SPELLED_VOLUME_WORD}\\s+)+(?:and\\s+(?:${SPELLED_VOLUME_WORD}|${SPELLED_HYPHEN_TEENS}-${SPELLED_HYPHEN_TEENS}))*\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_LABEL}(?:\\s+[A-Za-z]+){0,4}\\s*[:：]\\s*a\\s+${SPELLED_VOLUME_WORD}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_LABEL}${labelPad}\\s*[:：]\\s*(?:(?:HKD|USD|EUR|GBP|CNY|RMB|HK\\$)(?:\\s+(?:minus|negative))?|minus|negative|[€£$¥￥])\\s*${SPELLED_AMOUNT_TAIL}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    // Label + copula (+ modifiers) + amount
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_LABEL}${labelPad}\\s+${copula}${SPELLED_COPULA_MODIFIER}\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT_PHRASE_COPULA}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\bThe\\s+price\\s+${copula}${SPELLED_COPULA_MODIFIER}\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT_PHRASE_COPULA}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b(?:The\\s+)?(?:recommended\\s+)?(?:${SPELLED_PRICE_LABEL}|price)${priceProsePad}\\s+${copula}${SPELLED_COPULA_MODIFIER}\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT_PHRASE_COPULA}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b(?:${SPELLED_PRICE_LABEL})${labelPad}\\s+${copula}${SPELLED_COPULA_MODIFIER}\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT_PHRASE_COPULA}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b(?:SRP|Price|Retail\\s+price)\\s+${copula}${SPELLED_COPULA_MODIFIER}\\s+(?:a\\s+)?${SPELLED_AMOUNT_TAIL}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_LABEL}${labelPad}\\s+(?:is|was|are|were)\\s+${SPELLED_LISTING_COPULA}\\s+(?:a\\s+)?(?:${SPELLED_EQUALS_QUALIFIER_WORD_RE})?${SPELLED_AMOUNT_TAIL}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
  new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_LABEL}${labelPad}\\s+ranges\\s+(?:from|between)\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT_UNIT}\\s+(?:to|and)\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT_UNIT}\\.?\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    // amounts to / comes to / totals
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_LABEL}\\s+(?:amounts\\s+to|comes\\s+to|totals)\\s+(?:a\\s+)?(?:(?:${SPELLED_EQUALS_QUALIFIER_WORD_RE})|(?:(?:minus|negative)\\s+))?${SPELLED_AMOUNT_TAIL}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    // Volume labels + copula
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_VOLUME_LABEL}\\s+${copula}${SPELLED_COPULA_MODIFIER}\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT_PHRASE_COPULA}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_VOLUME_LABEL}${labelPad}\\s+${copula}${SPELLED_COPULA_MODIFIER}\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT_PHRASE_COPULA}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    // BV points
    new RegExp(
      `\\b(?:BV|IBV|UC|PBV|GBV|PIBV)\\s+points?\\s*[:：]\\s*${SPELLED_AMOUNT_TAIL}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b(?:BV|IBV|UC|PBV|GBV|PIBV)\\s+points?\\s+${copula}\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT_TAIL}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b(?:BV|IBV|UC|PBV|GBV|PIBV)\\s+points?\\s*=\\s*${SPELLED_AMOUNT_PHRASE}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    // Bare label + single spelled amount (incl. hyphen compounds)
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_LABEL}\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT_UNIT}(?!\\s+(?:and\\s+)?(?:${SPELLED_VOLUME_WORD}|\\d))\\.?\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    // Glued currency after copula or =
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_LABEL}\\s+(?:${SPELLED_ENGLISH_COPULA}|=)\\s*[€£$¥￥]${SPELLED_AMOUNT_UNIT}\\.?\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    // of / has a value of
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_LABEL}\\s+of\\s+(?:a\\s+)?(?:${SPELLED_EQUALS_QUALIFIER_WORD_RE})?${SPELLED_AMOUNT_TAIL}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    new RegExp(
      `\\b${SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_LABEL}\\s+has\\s+(?:a\\s+)?value\\s+of\\s+${SPELLED_AMOUNT_TAIL}\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
    // Long “dollars” phrase after label
    new RegExp(
      `\\b(?:SRP|Price|BV|IBV|UC|PBV|GBV|PIBV|Business\\s+Volume|Retail\\s+price|Wholesale\\s+price|Wholesale\\s+cost)\\s*(?:[:：]|\\b${SPELLED_ENGLISH_COPULA}\\s+)\\s*(?![\\d])(?:[a-z][\\w-]*\\s+){2,24}(?:hong\\s+kong\\s+)?dollars\\.?\\b`,
      'i',
    ),
  ];
}

const ENGLISH_SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_PATTERNS = buildEnglishSpelledCommercialPatterns();

/** @param {string} normalizedText output of normalizeForDetection */
export function detectSpelledEnglishCommercial(normalizedText) {
  const t = String(normalizedText ?? '');
  if (!t) return false;
  return ENGLISH_SPELLED_COMMERCIAL_PATTERNS.some((re) => re.test(t));
}```

## File: kb/lib/guard-normalize.js
```
/** Bounded decode layers for A1/A5 guards (percent + HTML entities). */

const NAMED_HTML_ENTITIES = {
  lt: '<',
  gt: '>',
  amp: '&',
  quot: '"',
  apos: "'",
  nbsp: ' ',
};

export function decodePercentEncoding(text) {
  return String(text ?? '').replace(/%([0-9a-fA-F]{2})/g, (_, h) =>
    String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h, 16)),
  );
}

export function decodeHtmlEntities(text) {
  return String(text ?? '')
    .replace(/&#(\d+);?/g, (_, n) => String.fromCharCode(Number(n)))
    .replace(/&#x([0-9a-fA-F]+);?/gi, (_, h) => String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h, 16)))
    .replace(
      /&([a-z]+);?/gi,
      (full, name) => NAMED_HTML_ENTITIES[name.toLowerCase()] ?? full,
    );
}

const TRANSFORMS = [
  (s) => decodePercentEncoding(s),
  (s) => decodeHtmlEntities(s),
  (s) => s.normalize('NFKC'),
];

/** ModSecurity-style convergence: percent → entities, max 3 rounds per branch. */
export function expandGuardHaystack(text) {
  const raw = String(text ?? '');
  if (!raw) return [''];
  const out = new Set([raw]);
  const queue = [raw];
  let depth = 0;
  while (queue.length && depth < 9) {
    const batch = queue.splice(0, queue.length);
    depth += 1;
    for (const cur of batch) {
      for (const fn of TRANSFORMS) {
        const next = fn(cur);
        if (next !== cur && !out.has(next)) {
          out.add(next);
          queue.push(next);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return [...out];
}

export function haystackMatchesPattern(text, pattern) {
  if (!text) return false;
  for (const variant of expandGuardHaystack(text)) {
    if (pattern.test(variant)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

export function commercialDetectionLayers(raw) {
  return expandGuardHaystack(raw);
}```

## File: kb/lib/eligibility.js
```
import { isForbiddenClaimClass, chunkFailsCommercialGuard } from './classify.js';
import { validateManifestRow } from './manifest-schema.js';

/**
 * Retrieval eligibility (plan §6): manifest + chunk filters for search/get.
 */
export function manifestRetrievalEligible(row) {
  if (!row || row.ingest_allowed !== true) return false;
  if (row.status !== 'active') return false;
  if (row.superseded_by) return false;
  if (isForbiddenClaimClass(row.claim_class)) return false;
  const v = validateManifestRow(row);
  if (!v.ok) return false;
  return true;
}

export function chunkRetrievalEligible(manifestRow, chunk) {
  if (!manifestRetrievalEligible(manifestRow)) return false;
  if (!chunk?.chunk_id || !chunk?.text) return false;
  if (isForbiddenClaimClass(chunk.claim_class)) return false;
  if (chunkFailsCommercialGuard(chunk, manifestRow)) return false;
  if (manifestRow.doc_version) {
    if (!chunk.doc_version || chunk.doc_version !== manifestRow.doc_version) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}```

## File: kb/lib/index-db.js
```
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { indexDbPath, manifestPath, chunksPath } from './paths.js';
import { readJsonl } from './jsonl.js';
import { chunkRetrievalEligible } from './eligibility.js';
import { buildFtsQuery } from './search-query.js';
import { detectCommercialNumeric, detectOrderUrl } from './classify.js';
import { stripObsidianWikilinks } from './chunk-markdown.js';

export function initSchema(db) {
  db.exec(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chunks (
      chunk_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      source_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      doc_title TEXT,
      doc_version TEXT,
      claim_class TEXT,
      text TEXT NOT NULL
    );
  `);
  db.exec(`
    CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chunks_fts USING fts5(
      chunk_id UNINDEXED,
      text,
      tokenize = 'unicode61'
    );
  `);
}

/** Production indexer — never indexes ineligible chunks (§7.1). */
export async function rebuildIndex(corpusRoot) {
  const manifestRows = await readJsonl(manifestPath(corpusRoot));
  const manifestById = new Map(manifestRows.map((r) => [r.source_id, r]));
  const chunks = await readJsonl(chunksPath(corpusRoot));

  const dbPath = indexDbPath(corpusRoot);
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(dbPath), { recursive: true });
  if (fs.existsSync(dbPath)) fs.unlinkSync(dbPath);

  const db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath);
  initSchema(db);
  const insert = db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO chunks (chunk_id, source_id, doc_title, doc_version, claim_class, text)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
  );
  const insertFts = db.prepare(`INSERT INTO chunks_fts (chunk_id, text) VALUES (?, ?)`);

  let indexed = 0;
  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    const manifest = manifestById.get(chunk.source_id);
    if (!chunkRetrievalEligible(manifest, chunk)) continue;
    insert.run(
      chunk.chunk_id,
      chunk.source_id,
      chunk.doc_title ?? manifest?.title ?? '',
      chunk.doc_version ?? manifest?.doc_version ?? '',
      chunk.claim_class,
      chunk.text,
    );
    let pathHint =
      manifest?.vault_rel_path ?? manifest?.rel_path ?? manifest?.source_path ?? '';
    pathHint = stripObsidianWikilinks(pathHint);
    const pathOk =
      pathHint &&
      !detectCommercialNumeric(pathHint) &&
      !detectOrderUrl(pathHint);
    const ftsText = [chunk.doc_title, pathOk ? pathHint : '', chunk.text].filter(Boolean).join('\n');
    insertFts.run(chunk.chunk_id, ftsText);
    indexed += 1;
  }
  db.close();
  return { indexed, total: chunks.length };
}

export function searchIndex(
  corpusRoot,
  query,
  { limit = 8, ftsMode = 'or', dropStopwords = false, sourceIdPrefix = null } = {},
) {
  const dbPath = indexDbPath(corpusRoot);
  if (!fs.existsSync(dbPath)) return [];
  const db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath, { readOnly: true });
  const q = query.trim();
  if (!q) {
    db.close();
    return [];
  }
  const ftsQuery = buildFtsQuery(q, { mode: ftsMode, dropStopwords });
  if (!ftsQuery) {
    db.close();
    return [];
  }
  const sql = sourceIdPrefix
    ? `
    SELECT c.chunk_id, c.source_id, c.doc_title, c.doc_version, c.claim_class,
           snippet(chunks_fts, 1, '[[', ']]', '…', 32) AS snippet
    FROM chunks_fts
    JOIN chunks c ON c.chunk_id = chunks_fts.chunk_id
    WHERE chunks_fts MATCH ? AND c.source_id LIKE ?
    LIMIT ?`
    : `
    SELECT c.chunk_id, c.source_id, c.doc_title, c.doc_version, c.claim_class,
           snippet(chunks_fts, 1, '[[', ']]', '…', 32) AS snippet
    FROM chunks_fts
    JOIN chunks c ON c.chunk_id = chunks_fts.chunk_id
    WHERE chunks_fts MATCH ?
    LIMIT ?`;
  const stmt = db.prepare(sql);
  const rows = sourceIdPrefix
    ? stmt.all(ftsQuery, `${sourceIdPrefix}%`, limit)
    : stmt.all(ftsQuery, limit);
  db.close();
  return rows;
}

export function listIndexedChunkIds(corpusRoot) {
  const dbPath = indexDbPath(corpusRoot);
  if (!fs.existsSync(dbPath)) return [];
  const db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath, { readOnly: true });
  const rows = db.prepare('SELECT chunk_id FROM chunks').all();
  db.close();
  return rows.map((r) => r.chunk_id);
}

export function getChunk(corpusRoot, chunkId) {
  const dbPath = indexDbPath(corpusRoot);
  if (!fs.existsSync(dbPath)) return null;
  const db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath, { readOnly: true });
  const row = db
    .prepare(
      `SELECT chunk_id, source_id, doc_title, doc_version, claim_class, text FROM chunks WHERE chunk_id = ?`,
    )
    .get(chunkId);
  db.close();
  return row ?? null;
}```

## File: kb/lib/retrieve.js
```
import { getCorpusRoot } from './paths.js';
import { searchIndex, getChunk as getChunkRow } from './index-db.js';
import { readJsonl } from './jsonl.js';
import { manifestPath } from './paths.js';
import { chunkRetrievalEligible } from './eligibility.js';
import {
  detectCommercialNumeric,
  detectOrderUrl,
  detectKbSearchBlockedQuery,
  identifierFailsKnowledgeGuard,
} from './classify.js';
import { sourceMatchesChapter } from './search-query.js';

export async function kbSearch(
  query,
  {
    limit = 8,
    corpusRoot = getCorpusRoot(),
    ftsMode = 'or',
    dropStopwords = false,
    chapter,
    requireChapter = false,
    requireObsidian = false,
    sourceIdPrefix = null,
  } = {},
) {
  const q = query.trim();
  if (!q) return [];
  if (detectKbSearchBlockedQuery(q)) return [];
  const prefix =
    sourceIdPrefix ?? (chapter && requireChapter ? `manual_ch_${chapter}_` : null);
  const hits = searchIndex(corpusRoot, q, {
    limit: limit * 3,
    ftsMode,
    dropStopwords,
    sourceIdPrefix: prefix,
  });
  const manifestRows = await readJsonl(manifestPath(corpusRoot));
  const manifestById = new Map(manifestRows.map((r) => [r.source_id, r]));
  const out = [];
  for (const hit of hits) {
    const manifest = manifestById.get(hit.source_id);
    const full = getChunkRow(corpusRoot, hit.chunk_id);
    if (!full) continue;
    if (!chunkRetrievalEligible(manifest, full)) continue;
    if (
      identifierFailsKnowledgeGuard(hit.chunk_id) ||
      identifierFailsKnowledgeGuard(hit.source_id) ||
      identifierFailsKnowledgeGuard(hit.doc_title)
    ) {
      continue;
    }
    if (chapter && requireChapter && !sourceMatchesChapter(hit.source_id, chapter)) continue;
    if (requireObsidian && !String(hit.source_id).startsWith('obs_')) continue;
    let snippet = hit.snippet ?? '';
    if (detectCommercialNumeric(snippet) || detectOrderUrl(snippet)) {
      const flat = (full.text ?? '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      snippet = flat.slice(0, 120);
      if (detectCommercialNumeric(snippet) || detectOrderUrl(snippet)) snippet = '…';
    }
    out.push({
      chunk_id: hit.chunk_id,
      source_id: hit.source_id,
      doc_title: hit.doc_title,
      doc_version: hit.doc_version,
      snippet,
    });
    if (out.length >= limit) break;
  }
  if (/\bobsolete\b/i.test(q) && !/\bcurrent\b/i.test(q)) {
    const staleOnly = out.filter((h) => {
      const full = getChunkRow(corpusRoot, h.chunk_id);
      const text = full?.text ?? '';
      return /\bobsolete\b/i.test(text) || /\bsuperseded\b/i.test(text);
    });
    if (staleOnly.length === 0) return [];
    return staleOnly.slice(0, limit);
  }
  if (requireObsidian && out.length > 1) {
    const boostPath = (sourceId) => {
      const rel = manifestById.get(sourceId)?.vault_rel_path ?? '';
      let score = 0;
      if (/\bb2b\b/i.test(q) && /02-b2b/i.test(rel)) score += 2;
      if (/template/i.test(q) && /(templates|02-b2b)/i.test(rel)) score += 1;
      if (/template/i.test(q) && /templates\//i.test(rel)) score += 3;
      if (/pitch/i.test(q) && /shopcom-pitch/i.test(rel)) score += 4;
      if (/pitch/i.test(q) && /tone/i.test(q) && /02-b2b/i.test(rel)) score += 2;
      if (/template/i.test(q) && /outreach/i.test(q) && /shopcom-pitch/i.test(rel)) score += 3;
      return score;
    };
    out.sort((a, b) => boostPath(b.source_id) - boostPath(a.source_id));
  }
  return out;
}

export async function kbGet(chunkId, { corpusRoot = getCorpusRoot() } = {}) {
  const row = getChunkRow(corpusRoot, chunkId);
  if (!row) return null;
  const manifestRows = await readJsonl(manifestPath(corpusRoot));
  const manifest = manifestRows.find((r) => r.source_id === row.source_id);
  if (!chunkRetrievalEligible(manifest, row)) return null;
  return {
    chunk_id: row.chunk_id,
    doc_title: row.doc_title,
    doc_version: row.doc_version,
    text: row.text,
  };
}```

## File: kb/lib/kb-search-run.js
```
import { detectKbSearchBlockedQuery } from './classify.js';
import { kbSearch } from './retrieve.js';
import { enhanceKbSearchQuery, inferKbSearchOptions, chaptersInQuery } from './query-enhance.js';
import { buildFtsQuery, extractContentTokens } from './search-query.js';

function mergeHits(...lists) {
  const seen = new Set();
  const out = [];
  for (const list of lists) {
    for (const h of list) {
      if (seen.has(h.chunk_id)) continue;
      seen.add(h.chunk_id);
      out.push(h);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

const TOKEN_SKIP = new Set([
  'from',
  'operator',
  'vault',
  'without',
  'numbers',
  'what',
  'how',
  'does',
  'the',
  'for',
  'this',
  'today',
  'week',
  'named',
  'catalog',
  'sku',
  'key',
  'terms',
  'context',
  'hong',
  'kong',
]);

function supplementalQueryTokens(userQuery) {
  return extractContentTokens(userQuery, { dropStopwords: true })
    .filter((t) => !TOKEN_SKIP.has(t.toLowerCase()))
    .slice(0, 8)
    .join(' ');
}

const ZH_ORDERING_ALIAS = /(?:超連鎖訂貨程序|訂貨程序)/;
const EN_ORDERING_BRIDGE = 'product ordering procedures manual chapter 15';

function rankOutreachTemplateHits(hits) {
  const score = (h) => {
    let s = 0;
    if (h.source_id === 'obs_0ac326d5cfe0') s += 10;
    if (h.chunk_id === 'obs_7370808e3a2c#6') s += 8;
    if (/b2b-prospect|templates/i.test(String(h.source_id))) s += 4;
    if (/02-b2b/i.test(String(h.source_id))) s += 2;
    return s;
  };
  return [...hits].sort((a, b) => score(b) - score(a));
}

function needsOrderingAliasBridge(userQuery, hits) {
  if (hits.length) return false;
  const q = String(userQuery ?? '');
  return (
    ZH_ORDERING_ALIAS.test(q) ||
    (/ch15/i.test(q) && /ordering policy|訂貨|equivalent/i.test(q))
  );
}

/** Shared kb_search execution (MCP + eval preflight). */
export async function runKbSearch(userQuery, { limit = 8 } = {}) {
  if (detectKbSearchBlockedQuery(userQuery)) {
    return { hits: [], aliasBridge: null };
  }
  const enhanced = enhanceKbSearchQuery(userQuery);
  const opts = inferKbSearchOptions(userQuery);
  const narrow = supplementalQueryTokens(userQuery);
  let hits = await kbSearch(enhanced, { limit, ...opts });
  if (narrow && narrow.toLowerCase() !== enhanced.toLowerCase()) {
    hits = mergeHits(
      await kbSearch(narrow, { limit: Math.max(limit, 8), ...opts }),
      hits,
    ).slice(0, limit);
  }
  if (opts.requireObsidian && /\bwithout numbers\b/i.test(userQuery)) {
    const andQ = buildFtsQuery(narrow || enhanced, { mode: 'and', dropStopwords: true });
    if (andQ) {
      hits = mergeHits(
        await kbSearch(andQ, { limit: 8, ...opts, ftsMode: 'and', dropStopwords: true }),
        hits,
      ).slice(0, limit);
    }
  }
  if (opts.requireObsidian && /template/i.test(userQuery) && /outreach/i.test(userQuery)) {
    hits = mergeHits(
      await kbSearch('獨特賣點 USP Shop.com 02-B2B restaurant', {
        limit: 8,
        ...opts,
        ftsMode: 'or',
        dropStopwords: false,
      }),
      await kbSearch('b2b-prospect outreach Templates', {
        limit: 8,
        ...opts,
        ftsMode: 'or',
        dropStopwords: false,
      }),
      hits,
    );
    const andQ = buildFtsQuery(supplementalQueryTokens(userQuery), {
      mode: 'and',
      dropStopwords: true,
    });
    if (andQ) {
      hits = mergeHits(
        await kbSearch(andQ, { limit: 8, ...opts, ftsMode: 'and', dropStopwords: true }),
        hits,
      );
    }
    hits = rankOutreachTemplateHits(hits);
  }
  if (!hits.length && opts.requireChapter && opts.chapter) {
    const andQ = buildFtsQuery(narrow || enhanced, {
      mode: 'and',
      dropStopwords: true,
    });
    if (andQ) {
      hits = await kbSearch(andQ, {
        limit,
        ftsMode: 'and',
        dropStopwords: true,
        chapter: opts.chapter,
        requireChapter: true,
      });
    }
  }
  const chapters = chaptersInQuery(userQuery);
  if (chapters.length > 1) {
    let merged = [];
    for (const ch of chapters) {
      merged = mergeHits(
        merged,
        await kbSearch(userQuery, {
          limit: 6,
          ftsMode: 'or',
          chapter: ch,
          requireChapter: true,
        }),
      );
    }
    hits = mergeHits(merged, hits).slice(0, limit);
  }
  if (
    /\bpreferred customer\b/i.test(userQuery) &&
    /\b(?:order|hk\.shop|authoritative)\b/i.test(userQuery)
  ) {
    hits = mergeHits(
      await kbSearch('SEC.4 preferred customer ordering website Business Center', {
        limit: 6,
        chapter: '22',
        requireChapter: true,
        ftsMode: 'or',
        dropStopwords: false,
      }),
      hits,
    ).slice(0, limit);
  }
  let aliasBridge = null;
  if (needsOrderingAliasBridge(userQuery, hits)) {
    aliasBridge = EN_ORDERING_BRIDGE;
    hits = await kbSearch(EN_ORDERING_BRIDGE, {
      limit: Math.max(limit, 8),
      ftsMode: 'or',
      dropStopwords: false,
      chapter: '15',
      requireChapter: true,
    });
  }
  return { query: userQuery, enhanced, options: opts, hits: hits.slice(0, limit), ...(aliasBridge ? { aliasBridge } : {}) };
}```

## File: kb/lib/search-query.js
```
const STOPWORDS = new Set([
  'a',
  'an',
  'the',
  'is',
  'are',
  'was',
  'were',
  'what',
  'when',
  'where',
  'who',
  'how',
  'does',
  'do',
  'our',
  'your',
  'for',
  'to',
  'in',
  'on',
  'at',
  'of',
  'and',
  'or',
  'with',
  'this',
  'that',
  'today',
  'hk',
  'hong',
  'kong',
]);

export function extractContentTokens(query, { dropStopwords = false } = {}) {
  return query
    .trim()
    .split(/\s+/)
    .map((t) => t.replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}_-]/gu, ''))
    .filter((t) => t.length >= 2)
    .filter((t) => !dropStopwords || !STOPWORDS.has(t.toLowerCase()));
}

/** @param {'or'|'and'} mode */
export function buildFtsQuery(query, { mode = 'or', dropStopwords = false } = {}) {
  const tokens = extractContentTokens(query, { dropStopwords });
  if (!tokens.length) return '';
  const quoted = tokens.map((t) => `"${t.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`);
  return quoted.join(mode === 'and' ? ' AND ' : ' OR ');
}

export function chapterFromPermittedEvidence(text) {
  const all = chaptersFromPermittedEvidence(text);
  return all.length ? all[0] : null;
}

/** All manual chapter numbers named in permitted_evidence (e.g. ch22 + ch25). */
export function chaptersFromPermittedEvidence(text) {
  if (!text) return [];
  const re = /\b(?:manual\s+)?ch(?:apter)?\s*(\d{1,2})\b|\bch\s*(\d{1,2})\b/gi;
  const out = new Set();
  let m;
  while ((m = re.exec(text))) {
    const n = parseInt(m[1] ?? m[2], 10);
    if (n) out.add(String(n));
  }
  return [...out];
}

export function evidenceWantsB2B(text) {
  return /02-b2b/i.test(text ?? '');
}

export function sourceMatchesChapter(sourceId, chapter) {
  if (!chapter) return true;
  const ch = String(chapter).replace(/^ch/i, '');
  return new RegExp(`manual_ch_${ch}_`, 'i').test(sourceId ?? '');
}

export function evidenceWantsObsidian(text) {
  return /obsidian|02-b2b|templates/i.test(text ?? '');
}```

## File: kb/lib/jsonl.js
```
import fs from 'node:fs';
import readline from 'node:readline';

export async function readJsonl(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return [];
  const rows = [];
  const rl = readline.createInterface({
    input: fs.createReadStream(filePath, 'utf8'),
    crlfDelay: Infinity,
  });
  for await (const line of rl) {
    const t = line.trim();
    if (!t) continue;
    rows.push(JSON.parse(t));
  }
  return rows;
}

export function writeJsonl(filePath, rows) {
  const body = rows.map((r) => JSON.stringify(r)).join('\n') + (rows.length ? '\n' : '');
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, body, 'utf8');
}

export function appendJsonl(filePath, rows) {
  const body = rows.map((r) => JSON.stringify(r)).join('\n') + (rows.length ? '\n' : '');
  fs.appendFileSync(filePath, body, 'utf8');
}```

/** Query-only flags for kb_search (no injected search phrases). */

export function enhanceKbSearchQuery(query) {
  return String(query ?? '').trim();
}

export function inferKbSearchOptions(query) {
  const low = String(query ?? '').toLowerCase();
  if (/q4 corporate revenue|revenue target|cure diabetes|opc-3 cure/i.test(low)) {
    return { ftsMode: 'and', dropStopwords: true, requireChapter: false };
  }
  if (/obsidian|02-b2b|templates\/\*\*/i.test(low)) {
    return { ftsMode: 'or', dropStopwords: false, requireObsidian: true, requireChapter: false };
  }
  if (/template/i.test(low) && /outreach|b2b/i.test(low)) {
    return { ftsMode: 'or', dropStopwords: false, requireObsidian: true, requireChapter: false };
  }
  if (/webcenter|internet.*policies/i.test(low)) {
    return { ftsMode: 'or', dropStopwords: false, chapter: '20', requireChapter: true };
  }
  if (/70%\s*rule|70 percent|seventy percent/i.test(low)) {
    return { ftsMode: 'or', dropStopwords: false, chapter: '16', requireChapter: true };
  }
  if (
    /definition of business volume|business volume.*definition|manual definition of business volume/i.test(
      low,
    )
  ) {
    return { ftsMode: 'or', dropStopwords: false, chapter: '25', requireChapter: true };
  }
  if (/\bpreferred customer\b/i.test(low) && /\b(ufo|unfranchise owner)\b/i.test(low)) {
    return { ftsMode: 'or', dropStopwords: false, chapter: '22', requireChapter: true };
  }
  if (/social ads|social media.*advertising|advertising claims on social/i.test(low)) {
    return { ftsMode: 'or', dropStopwords: false, chapter: '19', requireChapter: true };
  }
  if (/preferred customer|pc program/i.test(low)) {
    return {
      ftsMode: 'or',
      dropStopwords: false,
      chapter: '22',
      requireChapter: true,
    };
  }
  const chapters = [...low.matchAll(/\bchapter\s*(\d{1,2})\b|\bch\s*(\d{1,2})\b/g)].map(
    (m) => m[1] ?? m[2],
  );
  const unique = [...new Set(chapters)];
  if (unique.length === 1) {
    return {
      ftsMode: 'or',
      dropStopwords: false,
      chapter: unique[0],
      requireChapter: true,
    };
  }
  if (unique.length > 1) {
    return { ftsMode: 'or', dropStopwords: false, chapter: unique[0], requireChapter: false };
  }
  if (/訂貨程序|超連鎖訂貨/.test(query)) {
    return { ftsMode: 'or', dropStopwords: false, chapter: '15', requireChapter: true };
  }
  if (/ordering procedures|product ordering/i.test(low)) {
    return { ftsMode: 'or', dropStopwords: false, chapter: '15', requireChapter: true };
  }
  if (/retail vs wholesale/i.test(low) && /order/i.test(low)) {
    return { ftsMode: 'or', dropStopwords: false, chapter: '15', requireChapter: false };
  }
  if (/manual ch\s*19|\bch19\b/i.test(low)) {
    return { ftsMode: 'or', dropStopwords: false, chapter: '19', requireChapter: true };
  }
  return { ftsMode: 'or', dropStopwords: false };
}

export function chaptersInQuery(query) {
  const low = String(query ?? '').toLowerCase();
  const chapters = [...low.matchAll(/\bchapter\s*(\d{1,2})\b|\bch\s*(\d{1,2})\b/g)].map(
    (m) => m[1] ?? m[2],
  );
  if (/\bpreferred customer\b/i.test(query) && /\b(ufo|unfranchise owner)\b/i.test(low)) {
    return ['22', '25'];
  }
  return [...new Set(chapters)];
}```

## File: kb/lib/chunk-markdown.js
```
/** `02-[[B2B]]/foo` → `02-B2B/foo` for FTS and host snippets. */
export function stripObsidianWikilinks(markdown) {
  return String(markdown ?? '').replace(/\[\[([^\]|#]+)(?:#[^\]|]+)?(?:\|([^\]]+))?\]\]/g, (_, target, alias) =>
    alias?.trim() ? alias.trim() : target.trim(),
  );
}

/**
 * Heading-based markdown chunks (## and ### boundaries).
 * Child sections inherit ancestor heading lines for classification guards (plan §6).
 */
export function chunkMarkdown(markdown, { docTitle = '' } = {}) {
  const lines = stripObsidianWikilinks(markdown).replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').split('\n');
  const sections = [];
  const headingStack = [];
  let current = { heading: docTitle || '(root)', lines: [], ancestors: [] };

  for (const line of lines) {
    const hm = line.match(/^(#{1,3})\s+(.+)$/);
    if (hm) {
      const level = hm[1].length;
      if (current.lines.length > 0 || current.heading) {
        sections.push(current);
      }
      while (headingStack.length && headingStack[headingStack.length - 1].level >= level) {
        headingStack.pop();
      }
      const ancestors = headingStack.map((h) => h.line);
      headingStack.push({ level, line });
      current = { heading: hm[2].trim(), lines: [line], ancestors };
    } else {
      current.lines.push(line);
    }
  }
  if (current.lines.length > 0) sections.push(current);

  return sections
    .map((s) => {
      const body = s.lines.join('\n').trim();
      const prefix = (s.ancestors ?? []).join('\n').trim();
      const text = prefix ? `${prefix}\n${body}` : body;
      return { heading: s.heading, text: text.trim() };
    })
    .filter((s) => s.text.length > 0);
}```

## File: kb/lib/merge-corpus.js
```
/**
 * Merge ingest output without wiping other source types (0.8 Obsidian + 0.4 manuals).
 */
export function mergeBySourceType(existingManifest, existingChunks, nextManifest, nextChunks, sourceType) {
  const keptManifest = (existingManifest ?? []).filter((m) => m.source_type !== sourceType);
  const keptSourceIds = new Set(keptManifest.map((m) => m.source_id));
  const keptChunks = (existingChunks ?? []).filter((c) => keptSourceIds.has(c.source_id));
  return {
    manifest: [...keptManifest, ...nextManifest],
    chunks: [...keptChunks, ...nextChunks],
  };
}

export function mergeObsidianIntoCorpus(existingManifest, existingChunks, nextManifest, nextChunks) {
  return mergeBySourceType(existingManifest, existingChunks, nextManifest, nextChunks, 'obsidian_md');
}

export function mergeManualIntoCorpus(existingManifest, existingChunks, nextManifest, nextChunks) {
  return mergeBySourceType(existingManifest, existingChunks, nextManifest, nextChunks, 'manual_html');
}```

/** A5 poisoned-context isolation check (plan §7.1). */

import {
  flattenMessageContent,
  A5_DEPTH_MARKER,
  A5_UNINSPECTABLE_MARKER,
} from './message-content.js';
import {
  detectCommercialNumeric,
  detectOrderUrl,
  isBenignKnowledgeDocVersion,
  knowledgePayloadCommercialOrOrderLeak,
  ORDER_CFM_ACTION_PATTERN,
  normalizeForDetection,
  textCommercialOrOrderLeak,
} from './classify.js';
import { expandGuardHaystack, haystackMatchesPattern } from './guard-normalize.js';
import { getCorpusRoot } from './paths.js';
import { getChunk } from './index-db.js';

const SENTINEL_RE = /UNF_CANARY(?:_[A-Z0-9_]+)?(?:=|[\s;,]|$)/i;
const STRUCTURE_GUARD_RE = new RegExp(
  `${A5_DEPTH_MARKER.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')}|${A5_UNINSPECTABLE_MARKER.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')}`,
);

function rejectUninspectableStructure(text) {
  if (!text) return null;
  if (STRUCTURE_GUARD_RE.test(text)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'uninspectable_generation_structure' };
  }
  return null;
}
const COOKIE_LEAK_RE =
  /\bUNF_COOKIE\b|\bUNF_CANARY\b|CFID=|CFTOKEN=|session_secret|session[_-]?cookie|JSESSIONID=|PHPSESSID=|auth_token=|"(?:CFID|CFTOKEN|JSESSIONID|PHPSESSID|sessionCookie|session_cookie|cookie)"\s*:|(?:^|[\n\r])\s*Cookie\s*:|(?:^|[\n\r])\s*Set-Cookie\s*:|\bCookie\s*:\s*\S/i;
function normalizeForCanaryHaystack(text) {
  if (!text) return '';
  return String(text)
    .replace(/\\u([0-9a-fA-F]{4})/g, (_, h) => String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h, 16)))
    .normalize('NFKC')
    .replace(/\p{Cf}/gu, '');
}

function cookieOrCanaryLeak(text) {
  if (!text) return false;
  const layers = new Set([text, normalizeForCanaryHaystack(text)]);
  for (const v of expandGuardHaystack(text)) {
    layers.add(v);
    layers.add(normalizeForCanaryHaystack(v));
  }
  for (const layer of layers) {
    if (COOKIE_LEAK_RE.test(layer)) return true;
    if (/\bUNF_COOKIE\b/i.test(layer) || /\bUNF_CANARY\b/i.test(layer)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function cartOrAccountLeak(text) {
  return (
    haystackMatchesPattern(text, CART_HTML_RE) || haystackMatchesPattern(text, ACCOUNT_PAYLOAD_RE)
  );
}
const CART_HTML_RE =
  /<[^>]*\b(?:class|id)\s*=\s*(?:["'][^"']*cart[^"']*["']|cart[A-Za-z0-9_-]*)|Private cart contents|<(?:div|section|main|article|form)[^>]*\bcart\b|checkout[-_]html|\bcart\s+snippet\b/i;
const ACCOUNT_PAYLOAD_RE =
  /"(?:account|customer|portal)"\s*:\s*[\[{]|"(?:account_?id|customer_?id|display_name|dist_?id|membership_?status|portal_?status)"\s*:|"orders"\s*:\s*\[|"email"\s*:\s*"[^"]+@|\baccountId\s*:/i;

  /"lineItems"\s*:|"lineCount"\s*:|"retailTotalHkd"|"bvTotal"|"ibvTotal"|"wholesaleTotalHkd"/;
/** Cart tool payloads often use status+empty even without line-item fields. */
const CART_STATUS_JSON_RE =
  /"status"\s*:\s*(?:200|204)\b[^}]{0,160}"empty"\s*:|"empty"\s*:\s*(?:true|false)\b[^}]{0,160}"status"\s*:\s*(?:200|204)\b/;

  /(?:\brole:\s*tool\b|["']role["']\s*:\s*["']tool["'])[\s\S]{0,240}?\bunf_[a-z0-9_]+|\bunf_[a-z0-9_]+[\s\S]{0,240}?(?:\brole:\s*tool\b|["']role["']\s*:\s*["']tool["'])/i;
const OPERATIONAL_MCP_TOOL_RE = /\bunf_[a-z0-9_]+\b/i;
const OPERATIONAL_PROSE_RE =
  /\b(?:Quarter unavailable|Request failed|parcel arrives|arrives Tuesday|ETA\b)\b/i;
const TOOL_RESULT_MARK_RE = /"type"\s*:\s*"tool_result"|\btype:\s*tool_result\b/i;

const KB_TOOL_USE_ID_LOOSE_RE = /"tool_use_id"\s*:\s*"[^"]*kb[^"]*"/i;
const TOOL_ROLE_HTTP_ERROR_RE =
  /"role"\s*:\s*"tool"[\s\S]{0,800}?(?:"status"\s*:\s*(?:40[13]|50\d)|"error"\s*:\s*"(?:Forbidden|Unauthorized))/i;
const OPERATIONAL_TOOL_INNER_PROSE_RE =
  /\b(?:Quarter unavailable|Request failed|Order submitted|distributor queue|Inventory is available|Warehouse confirmed|pickup window|Member basket|checkout can proceed|Signed-in (?:UFO )?portal|portal summary for this account|Signed-in owner details|Delivery scheduled|cart subtotal was confirmed|Owner is active|Eligibility confirmed)\b/i;

const OPERATIONAL_STATUS_JSON_RE =
