function stripNestedEnvelopeFields(seg) {
  let s = seg;
  const nestedObj =
    /"(?:metadata|details|provenance|audit|content|output|result)"\s*:\s*\{(?:[^{}]|\{(?:[^{}]|\{[^{}]*\})*\})*\}/gi;
  const nestedArr =
    /"(?:metadata|details|provenance|audit|content|output|result)"\s*:\s*\[(?:[^\[\]]|\[(?:[^\[\]]|\[[^\]]*\])*\])*\]/gi;
  const nestedStr = /"(?:metadata|details|provenance|audit|content|output|result)"\s*:\s*"[^"]*"/gi;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    s = s.replace(nestedObj, '').replace(nestedArr, '').replace(nestedStr, '');
  }
  return s;
}

function tryParseJsonObject(text) {
  const start = text.indexOf('{');
  if (start < 0) return null;
  const end = balancedJsonObjectEnd(text, start);
  if (end < 0) return null;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(text.slice(start, end + 1));
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function toolNameFromRoleSegment(seg) {
  const head = seg.split('\n')[0].trim();
  const parsed = head.startsWith('{')
    ? tryParseJsonObject(head)
  : tryParseJsonObject(`{${head}}`);
  if (roleIsHistoryEnvelope(parsed?.role) && typeof parsed.name === 'string') return parsed.name;
  return '';
}

function toolInnerFromObject(obj) {
  for (const key of ['content', 'output', 'result']) {
    const v = obj?.[key];
    if (typeof v === 'string') return v;
    if (v != null) return JSON.stringify(v);
  }
  return '';
}

function explicitToolNameFromEnvelope(envelope) {
  const m = String(envelope ?? '').match(/"name"\s*:\s*"([^"]+)"/i);
  return m?.[1] ?? '';
}

function kbEnvelopeNamesKbTool(envelope) {
  const toolName = explicitToolNameFromEnvelope(envelope) || toolNameFromRoleSegment(envelope);
  if (toolName) return /^kb_(?:search|get)$/i.test(toolName);
  return KB_TOOL_USE_ID_STRICT_RE.test(envelope);
}

const KB_SEARCH_HIT_ALLOWED_KEYS = new Set([
  'chunk_id',
  'snippet',
  'text',
  'source_id',
  'doc_title',
  'doc_version',
]);

function kbSearchHitShape(row) {
  if (
    !row ||
    typeof row !== 'object' ||
    typeof row.chunk_id !== 'string' ||
    !row.chunk_id.length
  ) {
    return false;
  }
  if (Object.keys(row).some((key) => !KB_SEARCH_HIT_ALLOWED_KEYS.has(key))) return false;
  let hasBody = false;
  for (const field of ['snippet', 'text']) {
    if (!Object.hasOwn(row, field)) continue;
    const val = row[field];
    if (typeof val !== 'string') return false;
    if (!val.trim()) continue;
    hasBody = true;
    if (!kbCitationBodyAllowed(val)) return false;
    if (!citationBodyMatchesCorpus(row.chunk_id, val)) return false;
  }
  if (!citationMetadataMatchesCorpus(row.chunk_id, row)) return false;
  return hasBody || typeof row.source_id === 'string';
}

function normalizeCitationCompare(text) {
  return normalizeForCanaryHaystack(String(text ?? ''))
    .replace(/\[\[|\]\]/g, ' ')
    .replace(/…/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\.{2,}/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
}

function citationHeadAllowed(deduped, startIndex, canon) {
  const head = deduped.slice(0, startIndex);
  if (!head.length) return true;
  const headStr = head.join(' ');
  if (canon.includes(headStr)) return true;
  for (const w of head) {
    if (/^\d{1,4}$/.test(w) || (w.length <= 3 && /\d/.test(w))) {
      if (!canon.includes(w)) return false;
      continue;
    }
    if (w.length >= 4 && !canon.includes(w)) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function citationTailAllowed(deduped, endIndex, canon) {
  const tail = deduped.slice(endIndex);
  if (!tail.length) return true;
  const tailStr = tail.join(' ');
  if (canon.includes(tailStr)) return true;
  for (const w of tail) {
    if (/^\d{1,4}$/.test(w) || (w.length <= 3 && /\d/.test(w))) {
      if (!canon.includes(w)) return false;
      continue;
    }
    if (w.length >= 4 && !canon.includes(w)) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function citationShortFragmentOk(part, canon) {
  const t = part.trim();
  if (!t) return true;
  if (t.length >= 8) return false;
  if (/^\d{1,4}$/.test(t)) return canon.includes(t);
  if (t.length <= 3 && /\d/.test(t)) return canon.includes(t);
  if (!kbCitationBodyAllowed(t)) return false;
  return canon.includes(t);
}

function citationFragmentAllowed(part, canon) {
  if (!part.length) return true;
  if (!kbCitationBodyAllowed(part)) return false;
  if (part.length < 8) return citationShortFragmentOk(part, canon);
  if (canon.includes(part)) return true;
  return citationSoftWindowMatch(part, canon);
}

function citationSoftWindowMatch(cite, canon) {
  const soft = cite
    .replace(/\[\[|\]\]/g, '')
    .replace(/…/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
  const deduped = [];
  for (const w of soft.split(' ')) {
    if (!w) continue;
    if (deduped[deduped.length - 1] !== w) deduped.push(w);
  }
  if (deduped.length < 4) return false;
  for (let n = Math.min(10, deduped.length); n >= 4; n -= 1) {
    for (let start = 0; start <= deduped.length - n; start += 1) {
      const probe = deduped.slice(start, start + n).join(' ');
      if (
        probe.length >= 12 &&
        canon.includes(probe) &&
        citationHeadAllowed(deduped, start, canon) &&
        citationTailAllowed(deduped, start + n, canon)
      ) {
        return true;
      }
    }
  }
  return false;
}

function citationBodyMatchesCorpus(chunkId, body) {
  const row = getChunk(getCorpusRoot(), chunkId);
  if (!row?.text) return false;
  const canon = normalizeCitationCompare(row.text);
  const cite = normalizeCitationCompare(body);
  if (!cite.length) return false;
  if (canon.includes(cite)) return true;
  const ellipsisParts = String(body)
    .split(/…+/u)
    .map((p) => normalizeCitationCompare(p))
    .filter((p) => p.length > 0);
  if (ellipsisParts.length > 1) {
    return ellipsisParts.every((part) => citationFragmentAllowed(part, canon));
  }
  const ftsMarkers = /\[\[|…/.test(body);
  if (!ftsMarkers) return false;
  const citeTrim = cite.replace(/…+/gu, '').trim();
  if (citeTrim.length >= 12 && canon.includes(citeTrim)) return true;
  return citationSoftWindowMatch(cite, canon);
}

const CITATION_METADATA_FIELDS = ['doc_title', 'doc_version', 'source_id'];

function citationMetadataMatchesCorpus(chunkId, hit) {
  const row = getChunk(getCorpusRoot(), chunkId);
  if (!row) return false;
  for (const field of CITATION_METADATA_FIELDS) {
    const val = hit[field];
    if (val == null || val === '') continue;
    if (typeof val !== 'string') return false;
    if (!kbCitationBodyAllowed(val)) return false;
    const canon = normalizeCitationCompare(row[field] ?? '');
    const cite = normalizeCitationCompare(val);
    if (!canon || canon !== cite) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function kbCitationBodyAllowed(text) {
  if (typeof text !== 'string' || !text.trim()) return false;
  if (isBenignKnowledgeDocVersion(text)) return true;
  if (OPERATIONAL_TOOL_INNER_PROSE_RE.test(text) || OPERATIONAL_PROSE_RE.test(text)) return false;
  if (cookieOrCanaryLeak(text)) return false;
  if (detectCommercialNumeric(text) || detectOrderUrl(text)) return false;
  return true;
}

const KB_GET_ALLOWED_KEYS = new Set([
  'chunk_id',
  'text',
  'snippet',
  'source_id',
  'doc_title',
  'doc_version',
]);

function kbGetChunkShape(obj) {
  if (
    !obj ||
    typeof obj !== 'object' ||
    typeof obj.chunk_id !== 'string' ||
    !obj.chunk_id.length
  ) {
    return false;
  }
  if (Object.keys(obj).some((key) => !KB_GET_ALLOWED_KEYS.has(key))) return false;
  let hasBody = false;
  for (const field of ['text', 'snippet']) {
    if (!Object.hasOwn(obj, field)) continue;
    const val = obj[field];
    if (typeof val !== 'string') return false;
    if (!val.trim()) continue;
    hasBody = true;
    if (!kbCitationBodyAllowed(val)) return false;
    if (!citationBodyMatchesCorpus(obj.chunk_id, val)) return false;
  }
  if (!hasBody) return false;
  return citationMetadataMatchesCorpus(obj.chunk_id, obj);
}

function kbHitsWrapperAllowed(obj) {
  if (!obj || typeof obj !== 'object' || !Array.isArray(obj.hits)) {
    return false;
  }
  // A citation-shaped hit cannot authorize unrelated sibling output.
  if (Object.keys(obj).some((key) => key !== 'hits')) return false;
  if (obj.hits.length === 0) return true;
  return obj.hits.every((h) => kbSearchHitShape(h) && kbCitationBodyAllowed(h.snippet ?? h.text ?? ''));
}

function kbInnerLooksLikeRetrievalCitation(innerPayload) {
  if (innerPayload == null) return false;
  const t = String(innerPayload).trim();
  if (!t) return false;
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(t);
    if (parsed === null) return true;
    if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
      if (parsed.length === 0) return true;
      return (
        parsed.every(
          (row) =>
            kbGetChunkShape(row) ||
            (kbSearchHitShape(row) && kbCitationBodyAllowed(row.snippet ?? row.text ?? '')),
        )
      );
    }
    if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') {
      if (Object.hasOwn(parsed, 'hits')) return kbHitsWrapperAllowed(parsed);
      if (kbGetChunkShape(parsed)) return true;
      if (kbSearchHitShape(parsed) && kbCitationBodyAllowed(parsed.snippet ?? parsed.text ?? '')) {
        return true;
      }
    }
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
  return false;
}

function rejectOperationalToolProseInPlainText(text) {
  if (!text) return null;
  if (OPERATIONAL_STATUS_JSON_RE.test(text)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_status_json_in_generation_context' };
  }
  if (OPERATIONAL_TOOL_INNER_PROSE_RE.test(text)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_prose_in_generation_context' };
  }
  return null;
}

function kbToolInnerPayloadAllowed(envelope, innerPayload) {
  if (KB_TOOL_USE_ID_LOOSE_RE.test(envelope) && !KB_TOOL_USE_ID_STRICT_RE.test(envelope)) {
    return false;
  }
  if (!kbEnvelopeNamesKbTool(envelope)) return false;
  const innerHay = poisonHaystack(innerPayload);
  if (cookieOrCanaryLeak(innerHay)) return false;
  if (cartOrAccountLeak(innerHay)) return false;
  if (OPERATIONAL_MCP_TOOL_RE.test(innerHay)) return false;
  if (OPERATIONAL_TOOL_INNER_PROSE_RE.test(innerPayload)) return false;
  if (knowledgePayloadCommercialOrOrderLeak(innerPayload)) return false;
  return kbInnerLooksLikeRetrievalCitation(innerPayload);
}

function roleIsTool(role) {
  return typeof role === 'string' && /^(?:tool|function)$/i.test(role);
}

function roleIsHistoryEnvelope(role) {
  const r = typeof role === 'string' ? role.toLowerCase() : '';
  return r === 'tool' || r === 'function' || r === 'assistant';
}

function validateKbHistoryEnvelope(obj) {
  if (!roleIsHistoryEnvelope(obj?.role)) return null;
  const toolName = typeof obj.name === 'string' ? obj.name : '';
  if (!toolName) return roleIsTool(obj.role)
    ? { ok: false, reason: 'non_kb_tool_role_in_generation_context' } : null;
  if (!/^kb_(?:search|get)$/i.test(toolName)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'non_kb_tool_role_in_generation_context' };
  }
  for (const key of ['output', 'result']) {
    if (Object.hasOwn(obj, key) && obj[key] != null) {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'kb_tool_role_extra_output_field_in_generation_context' };
    }
  }
  if (typeof obj.note === 'string' && obj.note.trim() && obj.note !== '{') {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'kb_tool_role_note_in_generation_context' };
  }
  const allowedEnvelopeKeys = new Set([
    'role',
    'name',
    'content',
    'tool_call_id',
    'tool_use_id',
    'note',
    'type',
    'id',
  ]);
  for (const key of Object.keys(obj)) {
    if (allowedEnvelopeKeys.has(key)) continue;
    if (obj[key] != null && obj[key] !== '') {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'kb_tool_role_extra_field_in_generation_context' };
    }
  }
  const inner = toolInnerFromObject(obj);
  if (inner && !kbToolInnerPayloadAllowed(JSON.stringify(obj), inner)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'kb_tool_role_poisoned_payload_in_generation_context' };
  }
  return null;
}

const MAX_NESTED_JSON_STRING_DEPTH = 8;

function jsonStringMayHoldToolRole(text) {
  if (typeof text !== 'string') return false;
  const t = text.trim();
  if (!t.startsWith('{') && !t.startsWith('[')) return false;
  return TOOL_ROLE_MARK_RE.test(t);
}

function walkToolRolesInValue(value, depth = 0) {
  if (value == null) return null;
  if (depth > MAX_NESTED_JSON_STRING_DEPTH) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'unparseable_tool_role_in_generation_context' };
  }
  if (typeof value === 'string') {
    if (!jsonStringMayHoldToolRole(value)) return null;
    let parsed;
    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(value.trim());
    } catch {
      return rejectEmbeddedJsonToolRoles(value);
    }
    return walkToolRolesInValue(parsed, depth + 1);
  }
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    for (const item of value) {
      const hit = walkToolRolesInValue(item, depth);
      if (hit) return hit;
    }
    return null;
  }
  if (typeof value === 'object') {
    const direct = validateKbHistoryEnvelope(value);
    if (direct) return direct;
    for (const v of Object.values(value)) {
      if (v != null && (typeof v === 'object' || Array.isArray(v) || typeof v === 'string')) {
        const hit = walkToolRolesInValue(v, depth);
        if (hit) return hit;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
  return null;
}

function jsonStringStateAt(text, pos) {
  let inString = false;
  let escape = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < pos; i++) {
    const c = text[i];
    if (inString) {
      if (escape) escape = false;
      else if (c === '\\') escape = true;
      else if (c === '"') inString = false;
      continue;
    }
    if (c === '"') inString = true;
  }
  return inString;
}

function balancedJsonObjectEnd(text, start) {
  if (text[start] !== '{') return -1;
  let depth = 0;
  let inString = false;
  let escape = false;
  for (let i = start; i < text.length; i++) {
    const c = text[i];
    if (inString) {
      if (escape) escape = false;
      else if (c === '\\') escape = true;
      else if (c === '"') inString = false;
      continue;
    }
    if (c === '"') {
      inString = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (c === '{') depth += 1;
    else if (c === '}') {
      depth -= 1;
      if (depth === 0) return i;
    }
  }
  return -1;
}

function rejectEmbeddedJsonToolRoles(text) {
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    if (text[i] !== '{' || jsonStringStateAt(text, i)) continue;
    const end = balancedJsonObjectEnd(text, i);
    if (end < 0) continue;
    try {
      const hit = walkToolRolesInValue(JSON.parse(text.slice(i, end + 1)));
      if (hit) return hit;
    } catch {
      /* ignore non-JSON brace runs */
    }
    i = end;
  }
  return null;
}

function rejectTruncatedHistoryToolRoles(text) {
  const trimmed = String(text ?? '').trim();
  if (!TOOL_ROLE_MARK_RE.test(trimmed)) return null;
  if (!/"role"\s*:\s*"(?:tool|function)"/i.test(trimmed)) return null;
  if (!trimmed.startsWith('{')) return null;
  const end = balancedJsonObjectEnd(trimmed, 0);
  if (end < 0) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'unparseable_tool_role_in_generation_context' };
  }
  return null;
}

function rejectNonKbToolRolesOnText(text) {
  if (!text) return null;
  if (!TOOL_ROLE_MARK_RE.test(text)) return null;
  const truncated = rejectTruncatedHistoryToolRoles(text);
  if (truncated) return truncated;
  const trimmed = text.trim();
  if (trimmed.startsWith('{') || trimmed.startsWith('[')) {
    try {
      const hit = walkToolRolesInValue(JSON.parse(trimmed));
      if (hit) return hit;
    } catch {
      /* multi-document or fragment; line/segment scanners below */
    }
  }
  for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    const t = line.trim();
    if (!t || !TOOL_ROLE_MARK_RE.test(t)) continue;
    const braceAt = t.indexOf('{');
    if (braceAt >= 0) {
      const truncated = rejectTruncatedHistoryToolRoles(t.slice(braceAt));
      if (truncated) return truncated;
    }
    if (!t.startsWith('{') && !t.startsWith('[')) continue;
    let parsed;
    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(t);
    } catch {
      const embedded = rejectEmbeddedJsonToolRoles(t);
      if (embedded) return embedded;
      continue;
    }
    const walked = walkToolRolesInValue(parsed);
    if (walked) return walked;
  }
  const segments =
    text.match(
      /"role"\s*:\s*"(?:tool|function)"[\s\S]{0,1600}?(?="role"\s*:\s*"(?:tool|function|assistant|user|system)"|$)/gi,
    ) ?? [];
  for (const seg of segments) {
    if (!/"role"\s*:\s*"(?:tool|function)"/i.test(seg)) continue;
    const wrapped = seg.trim().startsWith('{') ? seg.trim() : `{${seg.trim()}}`;
    let obj;
    try {
      obj = JSON.parse(wrapped);
    } catch {
      const oneLine = !/\n/.test(seg.trim());
      if (oneLine && seg.trim().startsWith('{')) {
        return { ok: false, reason: 'unparseable_tool_role_in_generation_context' };
      }
      continue;
    }
    if (!roleIsHistoryEnvelope(obj?.role)) continue;
    const hit = validateKbHistoryEnvelope(obj);
    if (hit) return hit;
  }
  return rejectEmbeddedJsonToolRoles(text);
}

function rejectNonKbToolRoles(text) {
  const trimmed = text?.trim() ?? '';
  if (trimmed.startsWith('{') || trimmed.startsWith('[')) {
    try {
      const walked = walkToolRolesInValue(JSON.parse(trimmed));
      if (walked) return walked;
    } catch {
      /* fragment; layered scan below */
    }
  }
  const layers = new Set([text, normalizeForCanaryHaystack(text)]);
  for (const v of expandGuardHaystack(text)) {
    layers.add(v);
    layers.add(normalizeForCanaryHaystack(v));
  }
  for (const layer of layers) {
    if (!TOOL_ROLE_MARK_RE.test(layer)) continue;
    const hit = rejectNonKbToolRolesOnText(layer);
    if (hit) return hit;
  }
  return null;
}

const TOOL_RESULT_ALLOWED_KEYS = new Set([
  'type',
  'tool_use_id',
  'tool_call_id',
  'name',
  'content',
  'is_error',
]);

function walkToolResultExtras(value) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    for (const item of value) {
      const hit = walkToolResultExtras(item);
      if (hit) return hit;
    }
    return null;
  }
  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
    if (value.type === 'tool_result') {
      for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
        if (!TOOL_RESULT_ALLOWED_KEYS.has(key)) {
          return { ok: false, reason: 'tool_result_extra_field_in_generation_context' };
        }
      }
    }
    for (const v of Object.values(value)) {
      const hit = walkToolResultExtras(v);
      if (hit) return hit;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

const FUNCTION_CALL_OUTPUT_MARK_RE =
  /"type"\s*:\s*"(?:function_call_output|custom_tool_call_output)"/i;

function rejectUnresolvedGenerationOutputs(message) {
  if (!message || typeof message !== 'object') return null;
  if (message.type === 'function_call_output' || message.type === 'custom_tool_call_output') {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'function_call_output_in_generation_context' };
  }
  const role = message.role;
  const namedKb = typeof message.name === 'string' && /^kb_(?:search|get)$/i.test(message.name);
  if (!roleIsHistoryEnvelope(role) && !namedKb) {
    if (message.output != null && message.output !== '') {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'unresolved_generation_output_in_context' };
    }
    if (message.result != null && message.result !== '') {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'unresolved_generation_output_in_context' };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function rejectUnresolvedGenerationOutputsInText(text) {
  if (!text || !FUNCTION_CALL_OUTPUT_MARK_RE.test(text)) return null;
  const trimmed = text.trim();
  if (trimmed.startsWith('{') || trimmed.startsWith('[')) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(trimmed);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        for (const item of parsed) {
          const hit = rejectUnresolvedGenerationOutputs(item);
          if (hit) return hit;
        }
      } else {
        const hit = rejectUnresolvedGenerationOutputs(parsed);
        if (hit) return hit;
      }
    } catch {
      /* fragment scan below */
    }
  }
  for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    const t = line.trim();
    if (!t.startsWith('{') || !FUNCTION_CALL_OUTPUT_MARK_RE.test(t)) continue;
    try {
      const hit = rejectUnresolvedGenerationOutputs(JSON.parse(t));
      if (hit) return hit;
    } catch {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'function_call_output_in_generation_context' };
    }
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    if (text[i] !== '{' || jsonStringStateAt(text, i)) continue;
    const end = balancedJsonObjectEnd(text, i);
    if (end < 0) continue;
    try {
      const hit = rejectUnresolvedGenerationOutputs(JSON.parse(text.slice(i, end + 1)));
      if (hit) return hit;
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
    i = end;
  }
  if (FUNCTION_CALL_OUTPUT_MARK_RE.test(text)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'function_call_output_in_generation_context' };
  }
  return null;
}

export function scanGenerationMessagesToolResultExtras(generationMessages = []) {
  for (const message of generationMessages) {
    const unresolved = rejectUnresolvedGenerationOutputs(message);
    if (unresolved) return unresolved;
    const hit = walkToolResultExtras(message);
    if (hit) return hit;
  }
  return null;
}

function rejectToolResultEnvelopeExtras(text) {
  if (!text || !TOOL_RESULT_MARK_RE.test(text)) return null;
  for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (!trimmed.startsWith('{')) continue;
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(trimmed);
      const hit = walkToolResultExtras(parsed);
      if (hit) return hit;
    } catch {
      /* fall through to block scan */
    }
  }
  for (const block of text.match(/\{[^{}]{0,4000}\}/g) ?? []) {
    if (!/"type"\s*:\s*"tool_result"/i.test(block)) continue;
    let obj;
    try {
      obj = JSON.parse(block);
    } catch {
      continue;
    }
    if (obj?.type !== 'tool_result') continue;
    for (const key of Object.keys(obj)) {
      if (!TOOL_RESULT_ALLOWED_KEYS.has(key)) {
        return { ok: false, reason: 'tool_result_extra_field_in_generation_context' };
      }
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function rejectUnresolvedToolResults(text) {
  if (!text || !TOOL_RESULT_MARK_RE.test(text)) return null;
  const extra = rejectToolResultEnvelopeExtras(text);
  if (extra) return extra;
  const envelopeRe =
    /"type"\s*:\s*"tool_result"[\s\S]{0,3000}?"content"\s*:\s*"((?:\\.|[^"\\])*)"/gi;
  let saw = false;
  for (const m of text.matchAll(envelopeRe)) {
    saw = true;
    const inner = m[1].replace(/\\"/g, '"');
    const idMatch = m[0].match(/"(?:tool_use_id|tool_call_id)"\s*:\s*"([^"]+)"/i);
    const envelope = idMatch ? `"tool_use_id":"${idMatch[1]}" ${m[0]}` : m[0];
    if (!kbToolInnerPayloadAllowed(envelope, inner)) {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'unresolved_tool_result_in_generation_context' };
    }
  }
  if (!saw) {
    for (const block of text.match(/\{[^{}]{0,3000}\}/g) ?? []) {
      if (!TOOL_RESULT_MARK_RE.test(block)) continue;
      saw = true;
      const contentM = block.match(/"content"\s*:\s*"((?:\\.|[^"\\])*)"/);
      const inner = contentM?.[1]?.replace(/\\"/g, '"') ?? block;
      const idMatch = block.match(/"(?:tool_use_id|tool_call_id)"\s*:\s*"([^"]+)"/i);
      const envelope = idMatch ? `"tool_use_id":"${idMatch[1]}" ${block}` : block;
      if (!kbToolInnerPayloadAllowed(envelope, inner)) {
        return { ok: false, reason: 'unresolved_tool_result_in_generation_context' };
      }
    }
  }
  if (TOOL_RESULT_MARK_RE.test(text) && !saw) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'unresolved_tool_result_in_generation_context' };
  }
  return null;
}
const UNTRUSTED_GEN_BLOCK_RE =
  /"type"\s*:\s*"(?:embedded_resource|image|resource)"/;
const ORDER_ACTION_URL_RE = new RegExp(`(?:action=)?${ORDER_CFM_ACTION_PATTERN}`, 'i');

/** JSON.stringify double-escapes nested JSON in string fields; scan both layers. */
function tryUnescapeJsonString(text) {
  const trimmed = text.trim();
  if (!trimmed.startsWith('{') && !trimmed.startsWith('[')) return null;
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(trimmed));
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function embeddedJsonSubstrings(text) {
  const out = [];
  const re = /\{[^{}]{0,2048}\}/g;
  let m;
  while ((m = re.exec(text))) {
    const decoded = tryUnescapeJsonString(m[0]);
    if (decoded) out.push(decoded);
  }
  return out;
}

function poisonHaystack(text) {
  if (!text) return '';
  const layers = [text];
  if (/\\"/.test(text)) layers.push(text.replace(/\\"/g, '"'));
  if (/\\u[0-9a-fA-F]{4}/.test(text)) {
    layers.push(text.replace(/\\u([0-9a-fA-F]{4})/g, (_, h) => String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h, 16))));
  }
  const decoded = tryUnescapeJsonString(text);
  if (decoded) layers.push(decoded);
  for (const sub of embeddedJsonSubstrings(text)) layers.push(sub);
  const out = new Set();
  for (const layer of layers) {
    out.add(layer);
    const norm = normalizeForCanaryHaystack(layer);
    if (norm) out.add(norm);
    for (const v of expandGuardHaystack(layer)) {
      out.add(v);
      const vn = normalizeForCanaryHaystack(v);
      if (vn) out.add(vn);
    }
  }
  return [...out].join('\n');
}

function rejectCommercialInContext(text) {
  if (!text) return null;
  if (textCommercialOrOrderLeak(text)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_commercial_in_generation_context' };
  }
  return null;
}

function rejectOperationalEnvelope(joined) {
  const prose = rejectOperationalToolProseInPlainText(joined);
  if (prose) return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_prose_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (BARE_CART_EMPTY_JSON_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'cart_empty_json_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (CART_STATUS_JSON_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'cart_status_json_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (CART_MCP_JSON_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'cart_mcp_json_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (CART_MCP_TOOL_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'cart_mcp_tool_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (PRODUCT_MCP_JSON_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'product_mcp_json_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (FORM1000_MCP_JSON_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'form1000_mcp_json_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (FORM1000_MCP_TOOL_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'form1000_mcp_tool_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (URL_BUILD_TOOL_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'url_build_mcp_tool_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (ORDER_ACTION_URL_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'order_action_url_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (TOOL_ROLE_CART_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'tool_role_cart_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (TOOL_ROLE_OPERATIONAL_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'tool_role_operational_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (TOOL_ROLE_HTTP_ERROR_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'tool_role_http_error_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (OPERATIONAL_MCP_TOOL_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_mcp_tool_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (OPERATIONAL_PROSE_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_prose_in_knowledge_payload' };
  const unresolvedTool = rejectUnresolvedToolResults(joined);
  if (unresolvedTool) return unresolvedTool;
  if (UNTRUSTED_GEN_BLOCK_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'untrusted_generation_block' };
  return null;
}

function rejectOperationalContextEnvelope(ctx) {
  const prose = rejectOperationalToolProseInPlainText(ctx);
  if (prose) return prose;
  if (BARE_CART_EMPTY_JSON_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'cart_empty_json_in_generation_context' };
  if (CART_STATUS_JSON_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'cart_status_json_in_generation_context' };
  if (CART_MCP_JSON_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'cart_mcp_json_in_generation_context' };
  if (CART_MCP_TOOL_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'cart_mcp_tool_in_generation_context' };
  if (PRODUCT_MCP_JSON_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'product_mcp_json_in_generation_context' };
  if (FORM1000_MCP_JSON_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'form1000_mcp_json_in_generation_context' };
  if (FORM1000_MCP_TOOL_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'form1000_mcp_tool_in_generation_context' };
  if (URL_BUILD_TOOL_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'url_build_mcp_tool_in_generation_context' };
  if (ORDER_ACTION_URL_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'order_action_url_in_generation_context' };
  if (TOOL_ROLE_CART_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'tool_role_cart_in_generation_context' };
  if (TOOL_ROLE_OPERATIONAL_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'tool_role_operational_in_generation_context' };
  if (TOOL_ROLE_HTTP_ERROR_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'tool_role_http_error_in_generation_context' };
  if (OPERATIONAL_MCP_TOOL_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_mcp_tool_in_generation_context' };
  if (OPERATIONAL_PROSE_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_prose_in_generation_context' };
  if (UNTRUSTED_GEN_BLOCK_RE.test(ctx)) return { ok: false, reason: 'untrusted_generation_block' };
  return null;
}

function operationalContextEncodedBypassRisk(raw) {
  const text = String(raw ?? '');
  const obfuscated =
    /%[0-9a-fA-F]{2}/.test(text) ||
    /&(?:quot|#0*34|#x0*22|amp|lt|gt|apos|#0*39);/i.test(text);
  if (!obfuscated) return false;
  return (
    !TOOL_RESULT_MARK_RE.test(text) &&
    !FUNCTION_CALL_OUTPUT_MARK_RE.test(text)
  );
}

export function extractKnowledgePayload(toolArgs, generationMessages = []) {
  const parts = [];
  if (toolArgs) parts.push(JSON.stringify(toolArgs));
  for (const m of generationMessages) {
    const flat = flattenMessageContent(m?.content);
    if (flat) parts.push(flat);
  }
  return parts.join('\n');
}

/**
 * @param {object} opts
 * @param {boolean} [opts.isolationEnabled=true]
 * @param {boolean} [opts.deliberateBypass=false] test-only negative control
 */
/** Test/diagnostic: corpus binding for kb_get / kb_search history bodies. */
export function citationMatchesCorpus(chunkId, body) {
  return citationBodyMatchesCorpus(chunkId, body);
}

/** User-authored tool args only — excludes schema fields like limit that collide with proximity guards. */
export function userControlledKnowledgePayload(serializedPayload = '', rawArgs = {}) {
  const parts = [];
  if (rawArgs && typeof rawArgs === 'object') {
    if (typeof rawArgs.query === 'string') parts.push(rawArgs.query);
    if (typeof rawArgs.chunk_id === 'string') parts.push(rawArgs.chunk_id);
    if (parts.length) return parts.join('\n');
  }
  const raw = String(serializedPayload ?? '').trim();
  if (raw.startsWith('{')) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      const a = parsed?.arguments ?? {};
      if (typeof a.query === 'string') parts.push(a.query);
      if (typeof a.chunk_id === 'string') parts.push(a.chunk_id);
    } catch {
      /* fall through */
    }
  }
  return parts.join('\n');
}

export function scanPayloadForLeaks(serializedPayload, rawArgs = {}) {
  const parts = [serializedPayload];
  if (rawArgs && typeof rawArgs === 'object') {
    for (const v of Object.values(rawArgs)) {
      if (typeof v === 'string') parts.push(v);
    }
  }
  const rawJoined = parts.join('\n');
  const payloadToolRoles = rejectNonKbToolRoles(rawJoined);
  if (payloadToolRoles) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'non_kb_tool_role_in_knowledge_payload' };
  }
  const payloadFuncCallOutput = rejectUnresolvedGenerationOutputsInText(rawJoined);
  if (payloadFuncCallOutput) {
    const reason = payloadFuncCallOutput.reason.includes('knowledge')
      ? payloadFuncCallOutput.reason
      : payloadFuncCallOutput.reason.replace(/generation_context/g, 'knowledge_payload');
    return { ok: false, reason };
  }
  const plainProse = rejectOperationalToolProseInPlainText(rawJoined);
  if (plainProse) return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_prose_in_knowledge_payload' };
  const joined = poisonHaystack(rawJoined);
  if (SENTINEL_RE.test(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'unexpected_sentinel_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (cookieOrCanaryLeak(joined)) return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_secret_in_knowledge_payload' };
  if (cartOrAccountLeak(joined)) {
    if (haystackMatchesPattern(joined, CART_HTML_RE)) {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'cart_html_in_knowledge_payload' };
    }
    return { ok: false, reason: 'account_payload_in_knowledge_payload' };
  }
  const op = rejectOperationalEnvelope(joined);
  if (op) return op;
  return null;
}

export function assertKnowledgeIsolation(opts) {
  const {
    operationalContext = '',
    knowledgePayload = '',
    knowledgeUserPayload,
    isolationEnabled = true,
    deliberateBypass = false,
  } = opts;

  const userPayload =
    knowledgeUserPayload ??
    userControlledKnowledgePayload(knowledgePayload, opts.knowledgeArguments);
  const ctxHay = poisonHaystack(operationalContext);
  const payloadHay = poisonHaystack(knowledgePayload);
  const payloadUserHay = poisonHaystack(userPayload);
  const ctxToolRoles = rejectNonKbToolRoles(operationalContext);
  if (ctxToolRoles) return ctxToolRoles;
  const ctxFuncCallOutput = rejectUnresolvedGenerationOutputsInText(operationalContext);
  if (ctxFuncCallOutput) return ctxFuncCallOutput;
  const ctxToolResultExtras = rejectToolResultEnvelopeExtras(operationalContext);
  if (ctxToolResultExtras) return ctxToolResultExtras;
  const ctxUnresolvedTools = rejectUnresolvedToolResults(operationalContext);
  if (ctxUnresolvedTools) return ctxUnresolvedTools;
  if (operationalContextEncodedBypassRisk(operationalContext)) {
    const ctxHayEarly = poisonHaystack(operationalContext);
    const ctxFuncCallDecoded = rejectUnresolvedGenerationOutputsInText(ctxHayEarly);
    if (ctxFuncCallDecoded) return ctxFuncCallDecoded;
    const ctxUnresolvedDecoded = rejectUnresolvedToolResults(ctxHayEarly);
    if (ctxUnresolvedDecoded) return ctxUnresolvedDecoded;
  }
  const ctxPlainProse = rejectOperationalToolProseInPlainText(operationalContext);
  if (ctxPlainProse) return ctxPlainProse;
  const payloadFuncCallOutput = rejectUnresolvedGenerationOutputsInText(knowledgePayload);
  if (payloadFuncCallOutput) {
    const reason = payloadFuncCallOutput.reason.includes('knowledge')
      ? payloadFuncCallOutput.reason
      : payloadFuncCallOutput.reason.replace(/generation_context/g, 'knowledge_payload');
    return { ok: false, reason };
  }
  const payloadToolRoles = rejectNonKbToolRoles(knowledgePayload);
  if (payloadToolRoles) {
    const reason = payloadToolRoles.reason.includes('knowledge')
      ? payloadToolRoles.reason
      : payloadToolRoles.reason.replace(/generation_context/g, 'knowledge_payload');
    return { ok: false, reason };
  }
  const payloadPlainProse = rejectOperationalToolProseInPlainText(knowledgePayload);
  if (payloadPlainProse) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      reason: payloadPlainProse.reason.replace(/generation_context/g, 'knowledge_payload'),
    };
  }

  if (deliberateBypass || !isolationEnabled) {
    if (SENTINEL_RE.test(ctxHay) && SENTINEL_RE.test(payloadHay)) {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_sentinel_leaked_into_knowledge_payload' };
    }
    return { ok: false, reason: 'isolation_disabled_or_bypassed' };
  }

  if (SENTINEL_RE.test(ctxHay) && SENTINEL_RE.test(payloadHay)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_sentinel_leaked_into_knowledge_payload' };
  }
  if (SENTINEL_RE.test(ctxHay)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_sentinel_in_generation_context' };
  }
  if (cookieOrCanaryLeak(ctxHay)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_secret_in_generation_context' };
  }
  if (cartOrAccountLeak(ctxHay)) {
    if (haystackMatchesPattern(ctxHay, CART_HTML_RE)) {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'cart_html_in_generation_context' };
    }
    return { ok: false, reason: 'account_payload_in_generation_context' };
  }
  if (OPERATIONAL_ACCOUNT_OBJECT_RE.test(ctxHay)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_account_object_in_generation_context' };
  }
  let ctxOp = rejectOperationalContextEnvelope(ctxHay);
  if (ctxOp) return ctxOp;
  if (operationalContextEncodedBypassRisk(operationalContext)) {
    ctxOp = rejectUnresolvedGenerationOutputsInText(ctxHay);
    if (ctxOp) return ctxOp;
    ctxOp = rejectUnresolvedToolResults(ctxHay);
    if (ctxOp) return ctxOp;
  }
  const ctxCommercial = rejectCommercialInContext(ctxHay);
  if (ctxCommercial) return ctxCommercial;
  const ctxStruct = rejectUninspectableStructure(ctxHay);
  if (ctxStruct) return ctxStruct;
  if (SENTINEL_RE.test(payloadHay)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'unexpected_sentinel_in_knowledge_payload' };
  }
  if (cookieOrCanaryLeak(payloadHay)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_secret_in_knowledge_payload' };
  }
  if (cartOrAccountLeak(payloadHay)) {
    if (haystackMatchesPattern(payloadHay, CART_HTML_RE)) {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'cart_html_in_knowledge_payload' };
    }
    return { ok: false, reason: 'account_payload_in_knowledge_payload' };
  }
  if (OPERATIONAL_ACCOUNT_OBJECT_RE.test(payloadHay)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_account_object_in_knowledge_payload' };
  }
  const payloadOp = rejectOperationalEnvelope(payloadHay);
  if (payloadOp) return payloadOp;
  const payloadCommercial = rejectCommercialInContext(payloadUserHay);
  if (payloadCommercial) {
    return { ok: false, reason: 'operational_commercial_in_knowledge_payload' };
  }
  const payloadStruct = rejectUninspectableStructure(payloadHay);
  if (payloadStruct) return payloadStruct;
  return { ok: true };
}
```

## File: kb/ingest/obsidian.js
```
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import {
  getCorpusRoot,
  getVaultRoot,
  ensureCorpusDirs,
  manifestPath,
  chunksPath,
  obsidianAllowlistRel,
} from '../lib/paths.js';
import { chunkMarkdown } from '../lib/chunk-markdown.js';
import { classifyChunkText } from '../lib/classify.js';
import { readJsonl, writeJsonl } from '../lib/jsonl.js';
import { mergeObsidianIntoCorpus } from '../lib/merge-corpus.js';
import { rebuildIndex } from '../lib/index-db.js';
import { assertManifestRow } from '../lib/manifest-schema.js';

function stableId(prefix, key) {
  const h = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(key).digest('hex').slice(0, 12);
  return `${prefix}_${h}`;
}

function listMarkdownFiles(vaultRoot, relDirs) {
  const files = [];
  for (const rel of relDirs) {
    const base = path.join(vaultRoot, rel);
    if (!fs.existsSync(base)) continue;
    walk(base, rel, files);
  }
  return files.sort();
}

function walk(absDir, relFromVault, acc) {
  for (const name of fs.readdirSync(absDir)) {
    if (name.startsWith('.') || name.includes('conflict')) continue;
    const abs = path.join(absDir, name);
    const rel = path.join(relFromVault, name);
    const st = fs.statSync(abs);
    if (st.isDirectory()) walk(abs, rel, acc);
    else if (name.endsWith('.md')) acc.push({ abs, rel: rel.replace(/\\/g, '/') });
  }
}

export async function ingestObsidianAllowlist({
  vaultRoot = getVaultRoot(),
  corpusRoot = getCorpusRoot(),
  docVersion = 'vault-0.8',
} = {}) {
  ensureCorpusDirs(corpusRoot);
  const priorManifest = await readJsonl(manifestPath(corpusRoot));
  const priorChunks = await readJsonl(chunksPath(corpusRoot));
  const allowRel = obsidianAllowlistRel();
  const mdFiles = listMarkdownFiles(vaultRoot, allowRel);

  const manifest = [];
  const chunks = [];

  for (const { abs, rel } of mdFiles) {
    const base = path.basename(rel);
    if (/product-catalog/i.test(base)) {
      manifest.push(
        assertManifestRow({
          source_id: stableId('obs', rel),
          source_type: 'obsidian_md',
          vault_rel_path: rel,
          title: base.replace(/\.md$/, ''),
          locale: 'zh',
          product_family: null,
          sku_ids: [],
          canonical_for_topic: null,
          duplicate_of_source_id: null,
          claim_class: 'operator_synthesis',
          status: 'quarantine',
          ingest_allowed: false,
          doc_version: docVersion,
        }),
      );
      continue;
    }

    const md = fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8');
    const sourceId = stableId('obs', rel);
    manifest.push(
      assertManifestRow({
        source_id: sourceId,
        source_type: 'obsidian_md',
        vault_rel_path: rel,
        title: base.replace(/\.md$/, ''),
        locale: 'zh',
        product_family: null,
        sku_ids: [],
        canonical_for_topic: null,
        duplicate_of_source_id: null,
        claim_class: 'operator_synthesis',
        status: 'active',
        ingest_allowed: true,
        doc_version: docVersion,
      }),
    );

    const sections = chunkMarkdown(md, { docTitle: base });
    sections.forEach((sec, i) => {
      const blob = `${sec.heading}\n${sec.text}`;
      const claimClass = classifyChunkText(blob, { defaultClass: 'operator_synthesis' });
      chunks.push({
        chunk_id: `${sourceId}#${i}`,
        source_id: sourceId,
        text: sec.text,
        claim_class: claimClass,
        doc_type: 'obsidian_md',
        doc_title: sec.heading,
        doc_version: docVersion,
      });
    });
  }

  const merged = mergeObsidianIntoCorpus(priorManifest, priorChunks, manifest, chunks);
  writeJsonl(manifestPath(corpusRoot), merged.manifest);
  writeJsonl(chunksPath(corpusRoot), merged.chunks);
  const indexStats = await rebuildIndex(corpusRoot);

  return {
    manifestCount: merged.manifest.length,
    chunkCount: merged.chunks.length,
    obsidianManifest: manifest.length,
    obsidianChunks: chunks.length,
    filesScanned: mdFiles.length,
    indexStats,
  };
}```

  },
  { fixture: 'mixed-policy.md', includes: 'This item earns twenty-one IBV.', kind: 'commercial_numeric' },
  { fixture: 'mixed-policy.md', includes: 'Price is zero.', kind: 'commercial_numeric' },
  ...[
    'SRP amounts to 181', 'Price totals -29.99', 'BV comes to 0', '售價每瓶181元',
    'SRP: one hundred eighty-one Hong Kong dollars.',
    'IBV was 16', 'UC were -0.5',
    'PBV allocation on the current official schedule 50',
    '0 allocated units contribute toward GBV',
    'PIBV one two three four five six 16',
    '業績點數為１６', '獎勵業績點數本次為0', '16 是本次業績點數',
  ].map((includes) => ({ fixture: 'mixed-policy.md', includes, kind: 'commercial_numeric' })),
  { fixture: 'mixed-policy.md', includes: 'Price equals 181', kind: 'commercial_numeric' },
  { fixture: 'mixed-policy.md', includes: 'SRP value is 181', kind: 'commercial_numeric' },
  { fixture: 'mixed-policy.md', includes: 'SRP value equals 181', kind: 'commercial_numeric' },
  { fixture: 'mixed-policy.md', includes: 'SRP value of 181', kind: 'commercial_numeric' },
  { fixture: 'mixed-policy.md', includes: 'Price (USD): 29.99', kind: 'commercial_numeric' },
  { fixture: 'mixed-policy.md', includes: 'IBV points equal 16', kind: 'commercial_numeric' },
  { fixture: 'mixed-policy.md', includes: '### HK1410', kind: 'commercial_numeric' },
  { fixture: 'mixed-policy.md', includes: 'shopglobal.com/alice', kind: 'order_url' },
  { fixture: 'mixed-manual.html', includes: 'Shopping/Cart', kind: 'order_url' },
  { fixture: 'mixed-manual.html', includes: 'orders.unfBillShip', kind: 'order_url' },
  { fixture: 'mixed-manual.html', includes: 'orders.productReorder', kind: 'order_url' },
  { fixture: 'mixed-manual.html', includes: 'autoship.unfAutoShip', kind: 'order_url' },
  { fixture: 'mixed-manual.html', includes: 'BV | 12.5', kind: 'commercial_numeric' },
];

function oracleSpanDetectionHaystack(chunkText, includes) {
  const lines = chunkText.split('\n');
  const idx = lines.findIndex((ln) => ln.includes(includes));
  if (idx < 0) return includes;
  const block = [];
  for (let j = Math.max(0, idx - 3); j < idx; j++) {
    const t = lines[j].trim();
    if (/^#{1,6}\s/.test(t)) block.push(lines[j]);
  }
  block.push(lines[idx]);
  for (let j = idx + 1; j < Math.min(lines.length, idx + 6); j++) {
    const t = lines[j].trim();
    if (t.startsWith('##')) break;
    if (t) block.push(lines[j]);
  }
  return block.join('\n');
}

function independentSpanDetects(rule, chunkText) {
  const includes = rule.includes;
  if (rule.kind === 'order_url') {
    if (detectOrderUrl(includes)) return true;
    const line = chunkText.split('\n').find((ln) => ln.includes(includes)) ?? '';
    if (line && detectOrderUrl(line)) return true;
    if (line && /^#{1,6}\s/.test(line.trim())) {
      return detectOrderUrl(oracleSpanDetectionHaystack(chunkText, includes));
    }
    return false;
  }
  if (detectCommercialNumeric(includes)) return true;
  const line = chunkText.split('\n').find((ln) => ln.includes(includes)) ?? '';
  if (line && detectCommercialNumeric(line)) return true;
  if (line && /^#{1,6}\s/.test(line.trim())) {
    return detectCommercialNumeric(oracleSpanDetectionHaystack(chunkText, includes));
  }
  return false;
}

/** Labeled spans that only trigger inside fixture line/table/heading context (not on bare `includes`). */
export const ORACLE_REQUIRES_CHUNK_CONTEXT = new Set([
  '1,288',
  '### HK1410',
  'Shopping/Cart',
]);

const ORACLE_INDEPENDENT_MUST_ALLOW = [
  'Glossary: the SRP field in the compensation plan is defined without quoting live amounts.',
  'Appendix: PIBV one two three four five six seven 16.',
];

/**
 * Run plan §7.1 (A) md + (B) html fixtures through ingest → index → search/get.
 */
export async function runClassifierOracleFixtures(corpusRoot, repoRoot) {
  const mdPath = path.join(repoRoot, 'kb/fixtures/mixed-policy.md');
  const htmlPath = path.join(repoRoot, 'kb/fixtures/mixed-manual.html');
  const md = fs.readFileSync(mdPath, 'utf8');
  const html = fs.readFileSync(htmlPath, 'utf8');

  const manifest = [];
  const chunks = [];

  const mdSource = 'oracle_mixed_md';
  manifest.push(
    assertManifestRow({
      source_id: mdSource,
      source_type: 'obsidian_md',
      vault_rel_path: 'fixtures/mixed-policy.md',
      title: 'mixed-policy',
      locale: 'en',
      product_family: null,
      sku_ids: [],
      canonical_for_topic: null,
      duplicate_of_source_id: null,
      claim_class: 'operator_synthesis',
      status: 'active',
      ingest_allowed: true,
      doc_version: 'oracle',
    }),
  );

  chunkMarkdown(md, { docTitle: 'mixed-policy' }).forEach((sec, i) => {
    const claimClass = classifyChunkText(sec.text, { defaultClass: 'operator_synthesis' });
    chunks.push({
      chunk_id: `${mdSource}#${i}`,
      source_id: mdSource,
      text: sec.text,
      claim_class: claimClass,
      doc_title: sec.heading,
      doc_version: 'oracle',
    });
  });

  const htmlSource = 'oracle_mixed_html';
  manifest.push(
    assertManifestRow({
      source_id: htmlSource,
      source_type: 'manual_html',
      url: 'file://fixtures/mixed-manual.html',
      title: 'mixed-manual',
      locale: 'en',
      product_family: null,
      sku_ids: [],
      canonical_for_topic: null,
      duplicate_of_source_id: null,
      claim_class: 'policy',
      status: 'active',
      ingest_allowed: true,
      doc_version: 'oracle',
    }),
  );

  chunkManualHtml(html, { docTitle: 'mixed-manual' }).forEach((sec, i) => {
    chunks.push({
      chunk_id: `${htmlSource}#${i}`,
      source_id: htmlSource,
      text: sec.text,
      claim_class: sec.claim_class,
      doc_title: sec.heading,
      doc_version: 'oracle',
    });
  });

  writeJsonl(manifestPath(corpusRoot), manifest);
  writeJsonl(chunksPath(corpusRoot), chunks);
  const stats = await rebuildIndex(corpusRoot);

  const forbiddenChunks = chunks.filter(
    (c) => isForbiddenClaimClass(c.claim_class) || chunkFailsCommercialGuard(c),
  );
  const pitchHit = await kbSearch('advertising guidelines', { corpusRoot, limit: 5 });
  const priceHit = await kbSearch('DEMO 1288', { corpusRoot, limit: 5 });
  const bvHit = await kbSearch('Wholesale members shipping', { corpusRoot, limit: 5 });

  const flattenedRows = chunkManualHtml(html).map((r) => r.text);
  const flattenedBvClassified = flattenedRows.some(
    (t) => t.includes('BV') && detectCommercialNumeric(t),
  );

  const forbiddenGets = [];
  const forbiddenSearchBlocked = [];
  for (const c of forbiddenChunks) {
    forbiddenGets.push((await kbGet(c.chunk_id, { corpusRoot })) !== null);
    const token = c.text.split(/\s+/).slice(0, 4).join(' ').slice(0, 32);
    if (token) {
      const hits = await kbSearch(token, { corpusRoot, limit: 10 });
      forbiddenSearchBlocked.push(
        hits.some((h) => h.chunk_id === c.chunk_id),
      );
    }
  }

  const independentFailures = [];
  for (const rule of ORACLE_INDEPENDENT_MUST_BLOCK) {
    const matching = chunks.filter((c) => c.text.includes(rule.includes));
    if (!matching.length) {
      independentFailures.push(`missing_span:${rule.fixture}:${rule.includes}`);
      continue;
    }
    const spanBlocked = matching.some((c) => independentSpanDetects(rule, c.text));
    if (!spanBlocked) {
      independentFailures.push(`span_detector_miss:${rule.fixture}:${rule.includes}`);
    }
    for (const c of matching) {
      const classified =
        rule.kind === 'order_url'
          ? detectOrderUrl(c.text) || c.claim_class === 'order_url'
          : detectCommercialNumeric(c.text) ||
            c.claim_class === 'commercial_numeric' ||
            chunkFailsCommercialGuard(c);
      const getBlocked = (await kbGet(c.chunk_id, { corpusRoot })) === null;
      const searchTok = rule.includes.replace(/[<>,]/g, ' ').split(/\s+/).find((t) => t.length > 3) ?? rule.includes;
      const hits = await kbSearch(searchTok, { corpusRoot, limit: 12 });
      const searchBlocked = !hits.some((h) => h.chunk_id === c.chunk_id);
      if (!classified) independentFailures.push(`classifier_miss:${c.chunk_id}:${rule.kind}`);
      if (!getBlocked) independentFailures.push(`get_leak:${c.chunk_id}`);
      if (!searchBlocked) independentFailures.push(`search_leak:${c.chunk_id}`);
    }
  }

  const indexedIds = new Set(listIndexedChunkIds(corpusRoot));
  for (const span of ORACLE_INDEPENDENT_MUST_ALLOW) {
    const matching = chunks.filter((c) => c.text.includes(span));
    if (!matching.length) independentFailures.push(`missing_benign_span:${span}`);
    for (const c of matching) {
      const hits = await kbSearch(span.split(':')[0], { corpusRoot, limit: 12 });
      if (detectCommercialNumeric(c.text) || !indexedIds.has(c.chunk_id) ||
          (await kbGet(c.chunk_id, { corpusRoot })) === null ||
          !hits.some((hit) => hit.chunk_id === c.chunk_id)) {
        independentFailures.push(`benign_span_blocked:${c.chunk_id}`);
      }
    }
  }
  const indexedForbidden = [];
  for (const c of forbiddenChunks) {
    if (indexedIds.has(c.chunk_id)) {
      indexedForbidden.push(c.chunk_id);
      independentFailures.push(`indexed_forbidden:${c.chunk_id}`);
    }
  }
  const productionIndexExcludesForbidden =
    indexedForbidden.length === 0 && forbiddenChunks.length > 0;

  return {
    stats,
    forbiddenChunkCount: forbiddenChunks.length,
    pitchRetrieved: pitchHit.length > 0,
    commercialSearchEmpty: priceHit.length === 0,
    policyHtmlRetrieved: bvHit.length > 0,
    flattenedBvClassified,
    forbiddenGetBlocked: forbiddenGets.every((x) => x === false),
    forbiddenSearchBlocked: forbiddenSearchBlocked.every((x) => x === false),
    indexedForbiddenChunkIds: indexedForbidden,
    productionIndexExcludesForbidden,
    independentOraclePass: independentFailures.length === 0,
    independentOracleFailures: independentFailures,
  };
}
```

## File: kb/ingest/manual-html.js
```
import * as cheerio from 'cheerio';
import { classifyChunkText } from '../lib/classify.js';

/** Minimal manual HTML chunker (Phase 0.4 / oracle fixture path). */
export function chunkManualHtml(html, { docTitle = 'manual' } = {}) {
  const $ = cheerio.load(html);
  const chunks = [];
  $('p').each((i, el) => {
    let text = $(el).text().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    const href = $(el).find('a[href]').first().attr('href');
    if (href) text = `${text} ${href}`.trim();
    if (!text) return;
    const claimClass = classifyChunkText(text, { defaultClass: 'policy' });
    chunks.push({
      heading: `${docTitle}#p${i}`,
      text,
      claim_class: claimClass,
    });
  });
  $('table').each((i, el) => {
    const rows = [];
    $(el)
      .find('tr')
      .each((_, row) => {
        const cells = [];
        $(row)
          .find('td, th')
          .each((__, cell) => cells.push($(cell).text().trim()));
        if (cells.length) rows.push(cells.join(' | '));
      });
    const text = rows.join(' ; ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (!text) return;
    const claimClass = classifyChunkText(text, { defaultClass: 'policy' });
    chunks.push({
      heading: `${docTitle}#${i}`,
      text,
      claim_class: claimClass,
    });
  });
  if (chunks.length === 0) {
    const text = $('body').text().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (text) {
      chunks.push({
        heading: docTitle,
        text,
        claim_class: classifyChunkText(text, { defaultClass: 'policy' }),
      });
    }
  }
  return chunks;
}```

## File: kb/ingest/manual-chapters.js
```
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import * as cheerio from 'cheerio';
import {
  getCorpusRoot,
  ensureCorpusDirs,
  manifestPath,
  chunksPath,
} from '../lib/paths.js';
import { readJsonl, writeJsonl } from '../lib/jsonl.js';
import { classifyChunkText, detectCommercialNumeric, chunkFailsCommercialGuard } from '../lib/classify.js';
import { assertManifestRow } from '../lib/manifest-schema.js';
import { mergeManualIntoCorpus } from '../lib/merge-corpus.js';
import { rebuildIndex } from '../lib/index-db.js';
import { chunkManualHtml } from './manual-html.js';

const MANUAL_BASE = 'https://hkg-eng.documents.unfranchise.com';

/** Plan 0.4 starter set — DOCUMENTS.md */
export const MANUAL_CHAPTERS_04 = [
  { chapter: '15', slug: '/chapter-15-product-ordering-procedures-and-policies/', title: 'Product ordering' },
  { chapter: '16', slug: '/chapter-16-product-sales-and-distribution/', title: 'Sales & distribution' },
  { chapter: '19', slug: '/chapter-19-advertising-and-promotion/', title: 'Advertising' },
  { chapter: '20', slug: '/chapter-20-internet-and-webcenter-policies/', title: 'Internet / WebCenter' },
  { chapter: '22', slug: '/chapter-22-preferred-customer-program-policies-and-procedures/', title: 'PC program' },
  { chapter: '25', slug: '/chapter-25-key-definitions-and-terminology/', title: 'Definitions' },
];

function sanitizeManualPolicyText(text) {
  const lines = text.split('\n').filter((line) => !detectCommercialNumeric(line));
  return lines.join('\n').trim();
}

function stableManualId(chapter, slug) {
  return `manual_ch_${chapter}_${crypto.createHash('sha256').update(slug).digest('hex').slice(0, 8)}`;
}

export function chunkManualPageHtml(html, { docTitle = 'manual', chapter = '' } = {}) {
  const $ = cheerio.load(html);
  const main = $('article, main, .content, #content, body').first();
  const scope = main.length ? main : $('body');
  const chunks = [];
  let buf = [];
  let heading = docTitle;
  let currentHeadingLevel = 1;
  const headingStack = [];

  const headingBlobPrefix = () => {
    if (currentHeadingLevel <= 1) return '';
    const lines = [];
    for (let level = 2; level < currentHeadingLevel; level++) {
      const h = headingStack[level - 1];
      if (h) lines.push(`${'#'.repeat(level)} ${h}`);
    }
    return lines.length ? `${lines.join('\n')}\n` : '';
  };

  const flush = () => {
    const text = buf.join('\n').trim();
    if (!text) return;
    const headingLine =
      currentHeadingLevel > 1 ? `${'#'.repeat(currentHeadingLevel)} ${heading}` : heading;
    const blob = `${headingBlobPrefix()}${headingLine}\n${text}`;
    const claimClass = classifyChunkText(blob, { defaultClass: 'policy' });
    chunks.push({
      heading: `${chapter ? `ch${chapter} ` : ''}${heading}`,
      text: blob,
      claim_class: claimClass,
    });
    buf = [];
  };

  scope.find('h1, h2, h3, h4, p, table, li').each((_, el) => {
    const tag = el.tagName?.toLowerCase();
    const piece = $(el).text().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (!piece) return;
    if (tag?.startsWith('h')) {
      flush();
      const level = Math.min(6, Number.parseInt(tag[1], 10) || 1);
      currentHeadingLevel = level;
      headingStack.length = level;
      headingStack[level - 1] = piece;
      heading = piece;
    } else if (tag === 'table') {
      flush();
      const tableChunks = chunkManualHtml(`<table>${$(el).html()}</table>`, { docTitle: heading });
      const headingLine =
        currentHeadingLevel > 1 ? `${'#'.repeat(currentHeadingLevel)} ${heading}` : heading;
      const prefix = headingBlobPrefix();
      for (const tc of tableChunks) {
        const blob = prefix ? `${prefix}${headingLine}\n${tc.text}` : `${headingLine}\n${tc.text}`;
        chunks.push({
          ...tc,
          text: blob,
          claim_class: classifyChunkText(blob, { defaultClass: 'policy' }),
        });
      }
    } else {
      buf.push(piece);
    }
  });
  flush();

  if (chunks.length === 0) {
    return chunkManualHtml(html, { docTitle });
  }
  return chunks;
}

export async function fetchManualChapter(url, { timeoutMs = 60_000 } = {}) {
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const t = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, {
      signal: ctrl.signal,
      headers: { 'User-Agent': 'unfranchaise-kb-ingest/0.4 (operator local)' },
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`fetch ${url} → ${res.status}`);
    return await res.text();
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(t);
  }
}

export async function ingestManualChapters04({
  corpusRoot = getCorpusRoot(),
  docVersion = 'manual-0.4',
  cacheRaw = true,
} = {}) {
  ensureCorpusDirs(corpusRoot);
  const priorManifest = await readJsonl(manifestPath(corpusRoot));
  const priorChunks = await readJsonl(chunksPath(corpusRoot));
  const rawDir = path.join(corpusRoot, 'raw', 'manual');

  const manifest = [];
  const chunks = [];

  for (const ch of MANUAL_CHAPTERS_04) {
    const url = `${MANUAL_BASE}${ch.slug}`;
    const sourceId = stableManualId(ch.chapter, ch.slug);
    const html = await fetchManualChapter(url);

    if (cacheRaw) {
      fs.mkdirSync(rawDir, { recursive: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(rawDir, `ch-${ch.chapter}.html`), html, 'utf8');
    }

    manifest.push(
      assertManifestRow({
        source_id: sourceId,
        source_type: 'manual_html',
        url,
        chapter: ch.chapter,
        title: ch.title,
        locale: 'en',
        product_family: null,
        sku_ids: [],
        canonical_for_topic: `manual_ch_${ch.chapter}`,
        duplicate_of_source_id: null,
        claim_class: 'policy',
        status: 'active',
        ingest_allowed: true,
        doc_version: docVersion,
      }),
    );

    const manifestRow = manifest[manifest.length - 1];
    const sections = chunkManualPageHtml(html, { docTitle: ch.title, chapter: ch.chapter });
    const isDefinitionsChapter = ch.chapter === '25';
    sections.forEach((sec, i) => {
      let claimClass = sec.claim_class;
      if (isDefinitionsChapter && claimClass !== 'order_url') {
        claimClass = 'policy';
      }
      if (claimClass === 'order_url') return;
      let text = sanitizeManualPolicyText(sec.text);
      if (!text) return;
      if (isDefinitionsChapter) {
        claimClass = 'policy';
      } else {
        claimClass = classifyChunkText(text, { defaultClass: 'policy' });
        if (claimClass === 'order_url' || claimClass === 'commercial_numeric') return;
      }
      const row = {
        chunk_id: `${sourceId}#${i}`,
        source_id: sourceId,
        text,
        claim_class: claimClass,
        doc_type: 'manual_html',
        doc_title: sec.heading,
        doc_version: docVersion,
      };
      if (isDefinitionsChapter && chunkFailsCommercialGuard(row, manifestRow)) return;
      chunks.push(row);
    });
  }

  const merged = mergeManualIntoCorpus(priorManifest, priorChunks, manifest, chunks);
  writeJsonl(manifestPath(corpusRoot), merged.manifest);
  writeJsonl(chunksPath(corpusRoot), merged.chunks);
  const indexStats = await rebuildIndex(corpusRoot);

  return {
    chapters: MANUAL_CHAPTERS_04.length,
    manifestCount: merged.manifest.length,
    chunkCount: merged.chunks.length,
    manualChunks: chunks.length,
    indexStats,
  };
}```

## File: kb/lib/index-harness.js
```
/**
 * Test-only indexer: loads every chunk into FTS (including mis-labeled commercial).
 * Production CLI/MCP must not import this module (plan §7.1).
 */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { indexDbPath, manifestPath, chunksPath } from './paths.js';
import { readJsonl } from './jsonl.js';
import { initSchema } from './index-db.js';

export async function rebuildIndexHarness(corpusRoot) {
  const manifestRows = await readJsonl(manifestPath(corpusRoot));
  const manifestById = new Map(manifestRows.map((r) => [r.source_id, r]));
  const chunks = await readJsonl(chunksPath(corpusRoot));

  const dbPath = indexDbPath(corpusRoot);
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(dbPath), { recursive: true });
  if (fs.existsSync(dbPath)) fs.unlinkSync(dbPath);

  const db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath);
  initSchema(db);
  const insert = db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO chunks (chunk_id, source_id, doc_title, doc_version, claim_class, text)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
  );
  const insertFts = db.prepare(`INSERT INTO chunks_fts (chunk_id, text) VALUES (?, ?)`);

  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    const manifest = manifestById.get(chunk.source_id);
    insert.run(
      chunk.chunk_id,
      chunk.source_id,
      chunk.doc_title ?? manifest?.title ?? '',
      chunk.doc_version ?? manifest?.doc_version ?? '',
      chunk.claim_class,
      chunk.text,
    );
    const ftsText = [chunk.doc_title, chunk.text].filter(Boolean).join('\n');
    insertFts.run(chunk.chunk_id, ftsText);
  }
  db.close();
  return { indexed: chunks.length, total: chunks.length };
}```

## File: kb/lib/manifest-schema.js
```
import { identifierFailsKnowledgeGuard } from './classify.js';

const REQUIRED_STRING = ['source_id', 'source_type', 'title', 'locale', 'claim_class', 'status', 'doc_version'];

export function validateManifestRow(row) {
  const errors = [];
  if (!row || typeof row !== 'object') return { ok: false, errors: ['row_not_object'] };
  for (const k of REQUIRED_STRING) {
    if (typeof row[k] !== 'string' || !row[k].trim()) errors.push(`missing_${k}`);
  }
  if (typeof row.ingest_allowed !== 'boolean') errors.push('ingest_allowed_must_be_boolean');
  if (!Array.isArray(row.sku_ids)) errors.push('sku_ids_must_be_array');
  if (row.source_type === 'obsidian_md' && typeof row.vault_rel_path !== 'string') {
    errors.push('vault_rel_path_required');
  }
  if (row.source_type === 'manual_html' && typeof row.url !== 'string') {
    errors.push('url_required_for_manual_html');
  }
  if (!('product_family' in row)) errors.push('missing_product_family');
  if (!('canonical_for_topic' in row)) errors.push('missing_canonical_for_topic');
  if (!('duplicate_of_source_id' in row)) errors.push('missing_duplicate_of_source_id');
  if (identifierFailsKnowledgeGuard(row.source_id)) errors.push('source_id_poisoned');
  return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors };
}

export function assertManifestRow(row) {
  const v = validateManifestRow(row);
  if (!v.ok) throw new Error(`invalid_manifest: ${v.errors.join(',')}`);
  return row;
}```

## File: kb/lib/kb-env.js
```
/** Strip operational secrets from kb subprocess (plan §4.0 A5). */

import { haystackMatchesPattern } from './guard-normalize.js';

const DENY_PREFIXES = ['UNF_COOKIE', 'UNF_CANARY'];
const DENY_EXACT_KEYS = new Set([
  'HTTP_COOKIE',
  'COOKIE',
  'Cookie',
  'AUTHORIZATION',
  'Authorization',
  'AUTH_TOKEN',
  'auth_token',
]);

const ALLOWED_EXACT_KEYS = new Set([
  'PATH',
  'HOME',
  'USER',
  'SHELL',
  'TMPDIR',
  'TEMP',
  'TMP',
  'LANG',
  'LC_ALL',
  'LC_CTYPE',
  'TZ',
  'NODE_OPTIONS',
  'NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS',
  'NODE_ENV',
  'PWD',
  'NODE_PATH',
  'UNF_KB_CORPUS',
  'UNF_KB_OP_CONTEXT',
  'UNF_OBSIDIAN_VAULT_SHOPCOM',
]);

const ALLOWED_KEY_PREFIXES = ['LC_', 'npm_'];

const DENY_VALUE_RE =
  /\bUNF_COOKIE\b|\bUNF_CANARY\b|CFID=|CFTOKEN=|JSESSIONID=|PHPSESSID=|auth_token=/i;

function isDeniedKey(key) {
  if (DENY_EXACT_KEYS.has(key)) return true;
  if (/^UNF_KB_/i.test(key) && !ALLOWED_EXACT_KEYS.has(key)) return true;
  return DENY_PREFIXES.some((p) => key === p || key.startsWith(`${p}_`));
}

function isAllowedKey(key) {
  if (isDeniedKey(key)) return false;
  if (ALLOWED_EXACT_KEYS.has(key)) return true;
  return ALLOWED_KEY_PREFIXES.some((p) => key.startsWith(p));
}

export function sanitizeKnowledgeEnv(env = process.env) {
  const next = {};
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(env)) {
    if (!isAllowedKey(key)) continue;
    if (
      key !== 'UNF_KB_OP_CONTEXT' &&
      typeof value === 'string' &&
      haystackMatchesPattern(value, DENY_VALUE_RE)
    ) {
      continue;
    }
    next[key] = value;
  }
  return next;
}

export function applyKnowledgeEnvSanitize() {
  const sanitized = sanitizeKnowledgeEnv(process.env);
  for (const key of Object.keys(process.env)) {
    delete process.env[key];
  }
  Object.assign(process.env, sanitized);
}```

## File: kb/lib/kb-tool-capture.js
```
import {
  assertKnowledgeIsolation,
  scanGenerationMessagesToolResultExtras,
  scanPayloadForLeaks,
  userControlledKnowledgePayload,
} from './canary.js';
import { A5_UNINSPECTABLE_MARKER, flattenMessageContent } from './message-content.js';

function serializeGenerationMessage(m) {
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(m, (_, v) => (typeof v === 'bigint' ? String(v) : v));
  } catch {
    return A5_UNINSPECTABLE_MARKER;
  }
}

/** Last outbound kb tool invocation (for A5 harness + tests). */
let lastCapture = null;

export function captureKnowledgeToolCall(
  name,
  args,
  { operationalContext = '', generationMessages = [] } = {},
) {
  const payload = JSON.stringify({ tool: name, arguments: args });
  const generationText = generationMessages.map((m) => serializeGenerationMessage(m)).join('\n');
  const generationFlatten = generationMessages
    .map((m) => flattenMessageContent(m?.content))
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join('\n');
  const generationBoundary = [operationalContext, generationText, generationFlatten]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join('\n');
  lastCapture = {
    name,
    args,
    payload,
    operationalContext,
    generationMessages,
    generationBoundary,
    at: Date.now(),
  };
  const msgExtras = scanGenerationMessagesToolResultExtras(generationMessages);
  const leak = scanPayloadForLeaks(payload, args);
  const check =
    msgExtras ??
    leak ??
    assertKnowledgeIsolation({
      operationalContext: generationBoundary,
      knowledgePayload: payload,
      knowledgeUserPayload: userControlledKnowledgePayload(payload, args),
      knowledgeArguments: args,
      isolationEnabled: true,
    });
  return { capture: lastCapture, isolation: check };
}

export function getLastKnowledgeToolCapture() {
  return lastCapture;
}```

## File: kb/lib/message-content.js
```
/** Flatten MCP / chat message content for A5 isolation scans. */

export const A5_DEPTH_MARKER = '<<UNF_A5_DEPTH_EXCEEDED>>';
export const A5_UNINSPECTABLE_MARKER = '<<UNF_A5_UNINSPECTABLE_BLOCK>>';

const MAX_FLATTEN_DEPTH = 12;

function flattenToolUse(part) {
  const name = String(part.name ?? '');
  const input = part.input != null ? JSON.stringify(part.input) : '';
  return `${name} ${input}`.trim();
}

function flattenPlainObject(obj) {
  if (!obj || typeof obj !== 'object') return '';
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(obj);
  } catch {
    return A5_UNINSPECTABLE_MARKER;
  }
}

export function flattenMessageContent(content, depth = 0) {
  if (content == null || content === '') return '';
  if (depth > MAX_FLATTEN_DEPTH) return A5_DEPTH_MARKER;
  if (typeof content === 'string') return content;
  if (typeof content === 'number' || typeof content === 'boolean') return String(content);
  if (Array.isArray(content)) {
    const parts = content.map((part) => {
      if (part != null && typeof part === 'object' && !Array.isArray(part) && part.type == null) {
        return flattenPlainObject(part);
      }
      return flattenMessageContent(part, depth + 1);
    });
    if (parts.some((p) => p === A5_DEPTH_MARKER)) return A5_DEPTH_MARKER;
    if (parts.some((p) => p === A5_UNINSPECTABLE_MARKER)) return A5_UNINSPECTABLE_MARKER;
    return parts.filter(Boolean).join('\n');
  }
  if (typeof content === 'object') {
    if (content.type === 'text') {
      if (typeof content.text === 'string') return flattenMessageContent(content.text, depth + 1);
      if (content.text != null) return flattenPlainObject(content.text);
      return A5_UNINSPECTABLE_MARKER;
    }
    if (content.type === 'tool_use') {
      return flattenToolUse(content);
    }
    if (content.type === 'tool_result') {
      const fromContent = flattenMessageContent(content.content, depth + 1);
      const fromOutput = flattenMessageContent(content.output, depth + 1);
      return [fromContent, fromOutput].filter(Boolean).join('\n');
    }
    if (typeof content.content === 'string') {
      return flattenMessageContent(content.content, depth + 1);
    }
    if (Array.isArray(content.content)) {
      return flattenMessageContent(content.content, depth + 1);
    }
    if (
      content.type === 'image' ||
      content.type === 'resource' ||
      content.type === 'embedded_resource'
    ) {
      return A5_UNINSPECTABLE_MARKER;
    }
    if (content.type == null) {
      return flattenPlainObject(content);
    }
    if (content.type && content.type !== 'text') {
      return A5_UNINSPECTABLE_MARKER;
    }
    return '';
  }
  return String(content);
}```

## File: kb/lib/kb-handlers.js
```
import { kbGet } from './retrieve.js';
import { runKbSearch } from './kb-search-run.js';
import { captureKnowledgeToolCall } from './kb-tool-capture.js';
import { scanPayloadForLeaks } from './canary.js';
import { A5_UNINSPECTABLE_MARKER } from './message-content.js';

const KB_SEARCH_KEYS = new Set(['query', 'limit']);
const KB_GET_KEYS = new Set(['chunk_id']);

function rejectExtraOrLeakyArgs(args, allowed) {
  const leak = scanPayloadForLeaks(JSON.stringify(args), args);
  if (leak) return leak;
  const extra = Object.keys(args ?? {}).filter((k) => !allowed.has(k));
  if (extra.length) {
    return { ok: false, reason: `unexpected_tool_arguments:${extra.join(',')}` };
  }
  return { ok: true };
}

function normalizeOperationalContext(operationalContext) {
  if (operationalContext == null || operationalContext === '') return '';
  if (typeof operationalContext === 'string') return operationalContext;
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(operationalContext);
  } catch {
    return A5_UNINSPECTABLE_MARKER;
  }
}

function normalizeGenerationMessages(generationMessages) {
  if (generationMessages == null || generationMessages === '') return [];
  if (typeof generationMessages === 'string') {
    return [{ role: 'assistant', content: generationMessages }];
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(generationMessages)) return null;
  return generationMessages.map((m) => {
    if (typeof m === 'string') return { role: 'assistant', content: m };
    return m;
  });
}

/**
 * Shared MCP + integration test entry (plan A5 capture on real tool path).
 */
export async function handleKbTool(
  name,
  args = {},
  { operationalContext = '', generationMessages = [] } = {},
) {
  const opCtx = normalizeOperationalContext(operationalContext);
  const genMsgs = normalizeGenerationMessages(generationMessages);
  if (genMsgs === null) {
    return { isError: true, text: 'A5 isolation failed: invalid_generation_messages_shape' };
  }
  if (name === 'kb_search') {
    const pre = rejectExtraOrLeakyArgs(args, KB_SEARCH_KEYS);
    if (!pre.ok) {
      return { isError: true, text: `A5 isolation failed: ${pre.reason}` };
    }
    const q = args.query;
    if (typeof q !== 'string' || !q.trim()) {
      return { isError: true, text: 'query must be a non-empty string' };
    }
    let limit = Number(args.limit ?? 8);
    if (!Number.isFinite(limit)) limit = 8;
    limit = Math.min(20, Math.max(1, Math.trunc(limit)));
    const iso = captureKnowledgeToolCall(
      'kb_search',
      { query: q, limit },
      { operationalContext: opCtx, generationMessages: genMsgs },
    );
    if (!iso.isolation.ok) {
      return { isError: true, text: `A5 isolation failed: ${iso.isolation.reason}` };
    }
    const { hits } = await runKbSearch(q, { limit });
    return { isError: false, text: JSON.stringify(hits, null, 2) };
  }
  if (name === 'kb_get') {
    const pre = rejectExtraOrLeakyArgs(args, KB_GET_KEYS);
    if (!pre.ok) {
      return { isError: true, text: `A5 isolation failed: ${pre.reason}` };
    }
    const id = args.chunk_id;
    if (typeof id !== 'string' || !id.trim()) {
      return { isError: true, text: 'chunk_id must be a non-empty string' };
    }
    const iso = captureKnowledgeToolCall('kb_get', { chunk_id: id }, {
      operationalContext: opCtx,
      generationMessages: genMsgs,
    });
    if (!iso.isolation.ok) {
      return { isError: true, text: `A5 isolation failed: ${iso.isolation.reason}` };
    }
    const row = await kbGet(id);
    return { isError: false, text: JSON.stringify(row, null, 2) };
  }
  return { isError: true, text: `Unknown tool: ${name}` };
}```

const cli = path.join(repo, 'kb/cli.js');
const r = spawnSync(process.execPath, [cli, 'search', '__kb_env_test__'], {
  env: { ...process.env, UNF_COOKIE: 'CFID=1;CFTOKEN=2' },
  encoding: 'utf8',
});
assert.equal(r.status, 0, `cli exit 0: ${r.stderr}`);

const envCheck = spawnSync(
  process.execPath,
  [
    '-e',
    "import('./kb/lib/kb-env.js').then((m) => { m.applyKnowledgeEnvSanitize(); console.log(process.env.UNF_COOKIE ?? 'UNF_COOKIE_stripped'); })",
  ],
  {
    cwd: repo,
    env: { ...process.env, UNF_COOKIE: 'secret=1' },
    encoding: 'utf8',
  },
);
assert.match(envCheck.stdout, /UNF_COOKIE_stripped/, 'child env sanitized');

const allowEnvCheck = spawnSync(
  process.execPath,
  [
    '-e',
    "import { sanitizeKnowledgeEnv, applyKnowledgeEnvSanitize } from './kb/lib/kb-env.js'; const e = sanitizeKnowledgeEnv({ PATH: '/bin', HTTP_COOKIE: 'CFID=1', UNF_KB_OP_CONTEXT: 'ok', UNF_COOKIE: 'x' }); console.log(e.HTTP_COOKIE === undefined && e.UNF_COOKIE === undefined && e.UNF_KB_OP_CONTEXT === 'ok' ? 'allowlist_ok' : 'fail');",
  ],
  { cwd: repo, encoding: 'utf8' },
);
assert.match(allowEnvCheck.stdout, /allowlist_ok/, 'kb env allowlist drops cookie env names');

const serverSrc = fs.readFileSync(path.join(repo, 'kb-server.js'), 'utf8');
assert.match(
  serverSrc,
  /UNF_KB_OP_CONTEXT.*operationalContext/s,
  'kb-server wires UNF_KB_OP_CONTEXT into handleKbTool',
);

const ctxRed = spawnSync(process.execPath, [cli, 'search', 'advertising policy'], {
  env: {
    ...process.env,
    UNF_KB_OP_CONTEXT: 'UNF_COOKIE=1; Private cart contents',
  },
  encoding: 'utf8',
});
assert.notEqual(ctxRed.status, 0, 'CLI blocks poisoned UNF_KB_OP_CONTEXT (MCP host path)');

console.log('test-kb-mcp.mjs: all assertions passed');

// Structural cases: every label/verb pair, either proximity direction, and edges.
for (const label of ['SRP', 'Price', 'BV', 'IBV', 'UC', 'PBV', 'GBV', 'PIBV']) {
  for (const verb of ['amounts to', 'totals', 'comes to', 'was', 'were']) {
    assert.equal(classifyChunkText(`${label} ${verb} -0.5`), 'commercial_numeric');
  }
  for (const number of ['0', '1', '-16', '+.5', '１，２８８']) {
    for (const s of [
      `${label} allocation on the current official schedule ${number}`,
      `${number} allocated units contribute toward ${label}`,
    ]) assert.equal(classifyChunkText(s), 'commercial_numeric', s);
  }
}
for (const s of [
  '業績點數為１６',

  'PIBV one two three four five six 16',
  '16 one two three four five six GBV',
  `PIBV ${'longword'.repeat(1000)} 16`,
]) assert.equal(classifyChunkText(s), 'commercial_numeric', s);
